FitCloud is an enterprise multi-tenant Gym Management SaaS. Turborepo monorepo, pnpm workspaces.

## Apps & ports

| App | Path | Port | What it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| API | `apps/api` | 4000 | Express + TypeScript + Prisma + PostgreSQL + Redis + BullMQ. Serves BOTH the tenant portal and the admin portal under `/api/v1`. Swagger at `/api/docs`. |
| Tenant portal | `apps/tenant-web` | 3001 | Next.js 15 App Router. Subdomain-per-tenant (`{slug}.localhost:3001` in dev). Gym owners/staff. |
| Super Admin portal | `apps/super-admin` | 3002 | Next.js 15. Platform team only — separate auth plane. |
| Landing | `apps/landing-web` | — | Untouched scaffold. |
| Mobile | `apps/mobile` | — | Flutter (BLoC · Dio · GetIt · go_router), Android-verified. **Rebuilt from scratch** against the approved "FitCloud Kinetic" HTML design (dark theme, coral→violet gradient for staff, lime→teal for members) — supersedes the Prompt 51/52 4-role-theme implementation, which was scrapped. One binary, role-branching shells off a single `/home` route (`HomeRouterScreen`). All five roles built — Owner, Manager, Receptionist, Trainer (staff plane) and Member (its own `/portal/*` plane). See Prompts 53/54/55/56. |

## Backing services (docker-compose at repo root)

| Container | Port | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| gym-saas-postgres | **5433**→5432 | DB `gym_saas`, user/pass `gym`/`gym`. `docker exec gym-saas-postgres psql -U gym -d gym_saas -c "..."` |
| gym-saas-redis | 6379 | Cache, rate limits, permission sets, onboarding sessions, BullMQ. |
| gym-saas-mailpit | 1025 SMTP / **8025** web+API | All dev email. OTP via `curl -s "http://localhost:8025/api/v1/messages?limit=1"` → 6 digits in Subject. |

**Object storage** (`core/storage/`) is no longer a docker-compose container (MinIO was dropped). `env.storage.isConfigured` is true once `S3_BUCKET`/`S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` are set (real AWS S3 — `apps/api/.env.example`) — when unset (the default local-dev state), uploads are written to `apps/api/uploads/` and served back from `/uploads` (`core/storage/local-storage.util.ts`, mounted in `app.ts`). No container to start either way.

## Dev commands

```bash
# from each app dir (pnpm may need `corepack pnpm` on this machine)
pnpm run dev            # api: tsx watch · web: next dev
pnpm exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json
pnpm exec eslint src --max-warnings=0
pnpm run build
pnpm exec vitest run    # api only; globs src/**/*.spec.ts
pnpm run prisma:seed    # api; idempotent upserts
pnpm exec prisma migrate dev --name <name> --skip-seed
```

Gotchas: killing dev servers can leave orphaned `node.exe` listeners — kill by port (`Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 3001`). Running `next build` while `next dev` runs corrupts `.next` and kills the dev server. Env vars load once at API startup (restart after `.env` edits). After changing seed permissions, re-run seed AND consider Redis `FLUSHALL` for stale permission caches.

`kaushalgym` ("Kaushal Fitness Studio," Enterprise plan/TRIALING) was built via the real onboarding flow (`/onboarding/register` → OTP → `/select-plan` → `/create-tenant`), then fully fleshed out via the live API as a realistic-data reference tenant: full Gym Profile (legal name/GST/registration/description/contact/address/business hours/social links via `/settings/profile*`), 4 branches (the auto-created default branch repurposed as "Main," plus 3 more — Bandra/Powai/Thane — each with full address/contact/capacity), 3 membership plans (Monthly Basic/Quarterly Pro/Annual Elite), and per branch: 4 staff (1 Manager + 2 Trainers + 1 Receptionist, `PENDING_VERIFICATION` since seed data never clicks the real activation-email link — expected, not a bug), 5 members each with an assigned membership, attendance (3 members per branch checked in+out), and a payment (4 members per branch, CASH/UPI/CREDIT_CARD/BANK_TRANSFER, auto-generating the matching `MemberInvoice` + `Income` row). Totals: 1 tenant, 4 branches, 17 users, 20 members, 20 memberships, 12 attendance records, 16 payments/invoices/income rows. All driven through the real HTTP API (not raw SQL) so every side effect — permission grants, audit logs, auto-invoice/income generation, dashboard aggregates — is genuine, not hand-crafted. Verified live: tenant-web dashboard/branches/staff/members pages and the Super Admin Tenant 360 View (Prompt 28/28.5) all render this tenant correctly with zero console errors; the Super Admin's Payments/Invoices sections correctly show empty (that's *platform* billing — FitCloud↔tenant — a separate concern from this tenant's own member payments, which is what got seeded here).

One real bug found and fixed *during the seeding script itself*, not the app: the OTP-lookup step matched the first 6-digit substring in any Mailpit subject line for the recipient, which grabbed a stale invoice number from `kaushal.chauhan@rajyasabha.digital`'s inbox (reused from earlier same-session testing) instead of the actual verification code — fixed by requiring `"verification code"` in the subject and filtering to messages sent after the script's own register call. Not a product bug — the script's own OTP-fetch logic — but worth knowing if this same disposable-script pattern gets reused later.

## Prompt history (what exists)

1–4 — Scaffolding, docker, core API skeleton, coding standards (`docs/coding-guidelines/`).
5–6 — **Authentication** (tenant plane): register/login/OTP/2FA/refresh-rotation/lockout/password reset/email verify/sessions/logout-all; JWT RS256, audience `fitcloud-tenant-app`; RLS on all tenant tables.
7 — **Onboarding wizard**: 7-step registration (`/register`), Redis session, sandbox payment, automatic tenant provisioning, cross-origin token handoff via URL fragment → `/onboarding-complete`.
8 — **Billing platform**: plans, subscription lifecycle (Trial→Active→PastDue→Grace→Suspended→Cancelled/Expired), gateway provider pattern (sandboxed Stripe/Razorpay/PayPal), coupons, invoices, webhooks, BullMQ sweeps, billing UI under `/billing`.
9 — **Super Admin portal**: separate JWT plane (`fitcloud-admin-app`, `core/security/admin-jwt.service.ts`), ~16 `admin-*` API modules, full Next app. Admin & tenant JWTs cryptographically cross-reject.
10 — **Tenant portal shell**: sidebar/header/dashboard widgets, tenant resolve enriched (branding/subscription/featureFlags/defaultBranch), tenant notifications + Socket.IO, Redux slices, shared UI kit.
11 — **IAM** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §IAM): users/roles/permissions/invitations/sessions/profile/audit modules, permission engine with per-user GRANT/DENY overrides, branch access, invitation flow, full frontend under `/users` `/roles` `/permissions` `/invitations`, profile & sessions UI.
11.5 — **Expiry rescue path**: expired trial/subscription no longer dead-ends — login + billing endpoints stay open (`EXPIRY_EXEMPT_PREFIXES` in tenant.middleware), status screens link to real `/billing` checkout, renewal payment reactivates the tenant. Verified live end-to-end.
12 — **Gym Profile, Branding & Business Settings** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Settings, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `gym-settings`): new `settings` module — gym profile (legal/contact/address/geo/business-hours/social-links), business settings (currency/timezone/date-time-format/measurement-unit), branding (colors/theme/logo/favicon/login-bg/dashboard-banner/email-logo, all data-URL uploads), invoice settings (prefix/tax/payment-terms/footer), email settings, notification prefs (SMS config-only). New tables `TenantProfile`, `TenantInvoiceSettings`; extended `TenantSettings`/`TenantBranding`. Perms `settings:read` (new) / `settings:manage` (existing). Every write invalidates the tenant resolve cache. Sidebar entry "Gym Settings" → `/gym-settings/profile`. Verified live via curl (permissions, audit trail, cache invalidation, 8-endpoint regression) and full Playwright E2E (all 4 pages, cancel-discards-edits, post-branding dashboard reload, IAM regression) — zero console errors.
13 — **Staff Management** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Staff Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `staff`): new `staff` module scoped to the three staff types (Manager/Trainer/Receptionist — Owner already exists, Member is out of scope). New table `StaffProfile` (1:1 `User`, RLS'd) carries employeeId (auto-generate + editable), firstName/lastName, gender, DOB, joining date, address, notes, and employment info (type/salary/shift/weekly-off/work-status); `User` itself is reused for auth/status/contact/emergency-contact/avatar, `UserBranch`/`UserRole` reused for branch+role assignment. New perms `staff:view/create/update/delete/restore/activate/invite/assign-branch/assign-role`, all granted to MANAGER. Subscription staff-limit enforcement (`TenantLimit.maxManagers/maxTrainers/maxReceptionists/maxStaff`) added from scratch — no prior enforcement existed anywhere. Create Staff sends an activation email (reuses the invitation email template + PasswordReset token machinery, NOT the `UserInvitation` table, since the User row already exists at creation time) — staff sets their own password at `/staff-activation/{token}`, flipping PENDING_VERIFICATION→ACTIVE. Reset Password reuses `forgotPassword`'s internals. CSV export/import + bulk activate/deactivate/delete included. Sidebar entry "Staff" → `/staff` (distinct from the pre-existing "Staff & Access" IAM entry → `/users`). **Fixed a pre-existing bug found while testing this module**: the API's CORS `methods` whitelist (`app.ts`) was missing `PUT`, silently blocking every PUT-based browser action including the already-shipped IAM roles/branches assignment — added `PUT` to the list. Verified live via curl (full CRUD, invitation flow, subscription-limit rejection at the exact plan cap, bulk ops, audit trail, 9-endpoint regression) and full Playwright E2E (create→email→activate→login→edit→assign-role→suspend→restore→bulk-deactivate→dashboard/IAM regression) — zero console errors.
13.5 — **Sidebar "coming soon" placeholders**: the 10 nav items already gated by plan feature flags but with no page built yet (Members, Branches, Memberships, Attendance, Workout Plans, Diet Plans, Payments, Income, Expenses, Reports) 404'd on click — a pre-existing, project-wide gap (not new). Added a shared `components/coming-soon.tsx` + one placeholder `page.tsx` per route so every nav item resolves to something real. Superseded for `/members` and `/memberships` by Prompt 14 below.
13.6 — **Tenant provisioning data repair (ad hoc)**: a real tenant (`kaushalgym`) was found stuck half-provisioned — `tenant_settings`/`tenant_branding` existed but `branches`/`tenant_modules`/`tenant_limits`/`tenant_usage`/`subscriptions` did not (the onboarding saga in `tenant-provisioning.service.ts` creates these in sequence; something interrupted it after branding). Backfilled all missing rows on the Enterprise plan directly via SQL, then flushed the `tenant:slug:*`/`tenant:id:*` Redis cache keys so the fix was visible without a restart. One-off data fix, not a code change — if this recurs, the provisioning saga likely needs to run inside a single DB transaction instead of sequential awaits.
14 — **Member Management** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Member Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `members`): new `members` module — `Member` is a standalone record, NOT a `User` row (no invitation/login flow was requested for this prompt, unlike Staff). New tables `Member` (memberId auto-gen+editable, profile/medical/emergency-contact fields, QR token+image, soft-delete), `MembershipPlan` (the gym's own plan catalog — distinct from FitCloud's `SubscriptionPlan`), `Membership` (one row per period; renew/upgrade supersede the old row instead of mutating it, so Renewal History is just this member's rows), `MembershipFreeze` (Freeze History), `MemberDocument` (inline data-URL uploads, images downscaled client-side via the existing `fileToDataUrl`, non-images via new `fileToRawDataUrl`, both in `lib/image-to-data-url.ts`). Business rule "one active membership per member" enforced in `MemberService`. Trainer assignment reuses the Staff module's `User`+`TRAINER`-role query; branch assignment reuses `Branch`. QR codes generated server-side with the `qrcode` npm package (newly added — nothing existed for this before). New perms `members:view/create/update/delete/restore/import/export/assign-trainer/assign-membership`, granted to MANAGER (all) and RECEPTIONIST (view/create/update/import/export/assign-membership); reuses the pre-existing `memberships:manage` permission for the plan-catalog CRUD. Subscription member-limit enforcement (`TenantLimit.maxMembers`) added, same pattern as Prompt 13's staff limits. Sidebar "Members" (existing nav item, permission bumped from the old speculative `members:read` to the new `members:view`) and "Memberships" (was a coming-soon stub, now a real minimal plan-catalog page — needed so "Assign Membership Plan" has something to assign). Verified live via curl (full CRUD, membership assign/renew/upgrade/freeze/unfreeze lifecycle, member-limit rejection at the exact plan cap, bulk ops, CSV export/import, audit trail, 10-endpoint regression) and full Playwright E2E (plan create→member create→assign→freeze→unfreeze→renew→edit→QR→document upload→bulk-deactivate→dashboard/staff regression) — zero console errors.

13.5 — **Password-hashing perf fix**: `/auth/login` (and every other password hash/verify path — register, staff activation, password reset) was measured live at ~900-950ms while every other endpoint ran 25-110ms. Root cause: `bcryptjs` (pure-JS bcrypt) doing a single `compare()` at cost factor 12 takes ~550-900ms on its own on dev hardware — confirmed by direct benchmark, not a DB/query issue. Fix: swapped the sole choke point `core/security/password.service.ts` (plus the two other direct-bcrypt call sites, `admin-roles/services/admin-role.service.ts` and `prisma/seed.ts`) from `bcryptjs` to `@node-rs/bcrypt` (napi-rs, prebuilt binaries for win32/darwin/linux — no node-gyp/build-tools step, same `$2` hash format so existing hashes keep verifying), **and** lowered `BCRYPT_SALT_ROUNDS` default from 12 → 10 (`.env`/`.env.example`/`config/env.ts` zod default; min allowed is still 10). Net: new hashes verify in ~260-280ms instead of ~950ms. Note the cost factor is baked into each stored hash at creation time — existing users keep their old (slower) hash until their password changes; the 4 documented dev test-credential accounts (`dana.lee`, `sam.staff`, `invited.trainer`, `admin@fitcloud.com`) were rehashed at cost 10 with their same known passwords so the fix is visible immediately, credentials unchanged. `bcryptjs`/`@types/bcryptjs` removed entirely (no remaining references). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings)/build/vitest(51 passed) all green, plus live curl timing before/after on the running dev stack.

15 — **Membership Plans module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Membership Plans, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `members` feature additions): expands the `MembershipPlan` model from a Prompt 14 scaffold (name/duration/price/isActive) into a full plan catalog — added `planCode` (auto-gen + editable, unique per tenant), `category`, `durationValue`+`durationType` (DAYS/WEEKS/MONTHS/YEARS, calendar-aware via a new `addDuration()` helper — `durationDays` kept only as an approximate display cache), `joiningFee`/`taxPercentage`/`discountPercentage`, `displayOrder`, `notes`, soft-delete (`deletedAt` — didn't exist on this model before), Plan Features (gym/branch access, PT sessions, group classes, diet consultation, locker access, guest passes, freeze allowed + days limit), and Membership Rules (validity window, grace period, renewal window, auto-renewal, min/max age). New backend actions: Activate/Deactivate/Soft-Delete/Restore/Duplicate Plan, and on the member side Downgrade/Extend/Cancel Membership + a `/resume` alias for the existing `/unfreeze` (same underlying action, new name to match this module's vocabulary). New granular perms `memberships:view/create/update/delete/restore/assign/renew/upgrade/freeze` replace the old blanket `members:assign-membership` on all membership-lifecycle routes (kept seeded but now unused, same "leave the old key as a harmless legacy" pattern as Prompts 13/14) — MANAGER gets all 9, RECEPTIONIST gets view/assign/renew/upgrade/freeze only (plan-catalog CRUD stays a Manager decision, a deliberate tightening vs the old blanket `memberships:manage`), TRAINER gets view only. Business rules verified live: plan code + name uniqueness, inactive/deleted plans rejected on assign, minimum-age gate, and the renewal-window rule (a renew attempt correctly rejected outside its configured window). Frontend: full Membership Plan List/Create/Edit pages (search/pagination/sort/filters/status-toggle/duplicate/confirm-dialogs) under `/memberships`, plus Downgrade/Extend/Cancel actions and a Freeze→"Resume" rename added to the existing Member detail page's Membership card. Verified live via curl (full plan CRUD + all business rules + 11-endpoint regression) and full Playwright E2E (plan create→edit→deactivate/activate→duplicate→member create→assign→extend→upgrade→downgrade→freeze→resume→cancel→plan soft-delete→dashboard/members/staff regression) — zero console errors.

16 — **Attendance Management module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Attendance Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `attendance` feature): new `attendance` module — new table `attendance_records` (RLS'd via a hand-written migration, `20260721184500_enable_rls_attendance`) with memberId/branchId/checkInTime/checkOutTime/attendanceDate/method/deviceName/deviceId/status/notes/checkedInBy/checkedOutBy/soft-delete. `AttendanceMethod` enum ships `QR_CODE`/`MANUAL` plus four not-yet-wired values (`BIOMETRIC`/`FACE_RECOGNITION`/`NFC`/`RFID`) so future device integrations are additive, not a schema migration. Backend: Check-In/Check-Out (self-service, e.g. QR) and Manual Check-In/Manual Check-Out (front-desk, attributed to `checkedInBy`/`checkedOutBy`) are separate endpoint pairs sharing the same `performCheckIn`/`performCheckOut` service internals; Validate-QR looks up eligibility without recording a visit (backs the scanner UI's preview step); Get Today's/History/Member/Branch Attendance + Summary (check-ins/check-outs-today, currently-inside, peak-check-in-hour, 7-day trend); Update (correct times/notes/status) and soft-Delete. Business rules enforced in `AttendanceService`: active + non-frozen + non-expired-membership required to check in (`eligibility()`), one open check-in per member (`findOpenByMember`), check-out requires an open check-in, branch access enforced via the existing `getBranchAccess`/`requireBranchAccess`, tenant timezone honored via a new `dateInTimezone()`/`hourInTimezone()` util (`Intl.DateTimeFormat`, zero new deps) so `attendanceDate` and the "peak hour" stat are stamped in the **branch's own** IANA timezone rather than server/UTC time. New perms `attendance:view/checkin/checkout/update/delete/export` (distinct from the pre-existing speculative `attendance:create`/`attendance:read`, left seeded but unused — same precedent as Membership Plans superseding the old blanket `memberships:manage`) — MANAGER gets all 6, RECEPTIONIST gets view/checkin/checkout/export (front-desk only — record correction/deletion stays Manager-only), TRAINER gets view/checkin/checkout only. Real-time: reuses the existing Socket.IO plumbing exactly as instructed — `emitToTenant(tenantId, 'attendance:checkin'|'attendance:checkout', dto)` on every successful mutation, and the app-wide `RealtimeProvider` (already listening for these two event names from an earlier prompt) invalidates the `['attendance']` query-key prefix, so the dashboard's summary cards/today list/history update live in every open tab with no polling. Frontend: Attendance Dashboard (`/attendance`, summary cards + trend chart + today's activity feed + branch filter), Check In/Out (`/attendance/check-in`, a QR-scanner panel via `getUserMedia` + a manual-search panel sharing the eligibility/reason logic with the backend's own rules for instant feedback before submitting), History (`/attendance/history`, search/pagination/sort/date+branch+status filters/CSV+Excel export), and a "Recent visits" card added to the existing Member detail page. **Bug found + fixed during live real-time testing**: `getSummary`/`getToday` computed "today" using hardcoded `'UTC'` whenever no specific branch filter was applied (the dashboard's default, all-branches view), while `performCheckIn` always stamps `attendanceDate` using the **member's actual branch** timezone — for any branch not on UTC this silently undercounted "Check-ins today" on the all-branches dashboard even though "Currently inside" (which isn't date-bucketed) updated correctly. Fixed by adding a `defaultTimezone()` helper (tenant's `isDefault` branch, same ordering convention as `BranchRepository.list()`) as the fallback instead of `'UTC'`. Also fixed an unrelated hydration bug on the dashboard's empty-state tip (a `Badge` — which renders a `<div>` — was nested inside a `<p>`; changed the wrapper to a `<div>`). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `attendance.validators.spec.ts`, 16 tests, full API suite 97/97 green) all pass, plus live Playwright E2E covering QR-validate→check-in/out, manual check-in/out, duplicate check-in correctly rejected with 409, History search, Member-detail "Recent visits" card, and a two-tab real-time test proving the dashboard's "Currently inside"/"Check-ins today" counters update live in a second tab with no reload — zero unexpected console errors (the 409 from the intentional duplicate-check-in test is expected).

17 — **Workout Management module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Workout Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `workouts` feature): new `workouts` module — five new tables, all RLS'd via a hand-written migration (`20260723043400_enable_rls_workout_management`, right after the model migration `20260723043335_workout_management_module`): `Exercise` (the reusable exercise library — name/category/muscleGroup/equipment/difficultyLevel/instructions/image (data-URL)/videoUrl (reference-only, never fetched)/duration/defaultSets/defaultReps/restSeconds/caloriesBurnEstimate/soft-delete), `WorkoutPlan` (the gym's reusable plan catalog, same shape as `MembershipPlan` — name/description/goal/level/durationWeeks/trainerId/soft-delete), `WorkoutPlanExercise` (the weekly schedule — one row per exercise placed on a `dayOfWeek`, `sortOrder` is the drag-and-drop position within that day), `MemberWorkoutPlan` (one row per assignment — assigning a new/same plan while one is `ACTIVE` supersedes the old row to `COMPLETED`, same "supersede, never mutate" history pattern as `Membership`; `Removed` via a `CANCELLED` status transition, never deleted), `MemberWorkoutProgress` (per-exercise completion state, unique per assignment+exercise — marking progress again just overwrites the row's status/notes/timestamp, this is "Completed Exercises"/"Skipped Exercises"/progress-percent all from one table). New perms `workouts:view/create/update/delete/restore/assign/progress` (distinct from the pre-existing speculative `workout-plans:manage`, left seeded but unused, same precedent as every prior module) — MANAGER gets all 7, TRAINER gets view/create/update/assign/progress (no delete/restore — catalog deletion stays Manager-only), RECEPTIONIST gets view only. Business rule "only active trainers can create or manage workout plans" enforced via `assertTrainerActive()` (mirrors the pre-existing `assertTrainerValid` pattern in `MemberService`, but additionally checks `status === 'ACTIVE'`, which the original didn't) whenever `trainerId` is set/changed. Frontend: Workout Plan List/Create/Edit (`/workout-plans`, `/workout-plans/new`, `/workout-plans/[planId]` — the edit page also hosts the weekly-schedule editor and an "assign to a member" panel reusing the Attendance module's `MemberCheckinSearch` component), Exercise Library (`/workout-plans/exercises`, list + Create/Edit via a Dialog rather than separate routes), and a new "Workout" card on the Member detail page (current assignment + per-exercise Done/Skip buttons + Assign/Remove + a collapsible plan history). Drag-and-drop exercise ordering uses native HTML5 `draggable`/`onDragOver`/`onDrop` — no new npm dependency. **Bug found + fixed during live testing**: `WorkoutPlanRepository.replaceExercises()` originally batched the delete+creates in one `$transaction([...])` array — but the tenant-scoped Prisma client (`getTenantScopedClient`) already wraps every individual call in its OWN nested `set_config`+query transaction for RLS session-variable scoping, and nesting a batched `$transaction` on top of that silently dropped every write (the endpoint returned success, but nothing persisted — caught because a plan's exercises read back as empty after a "successful" save, and confirmed further because a subsequent Duplicate copied zero exercises). Fixed by rewriting it as plain sequential `await`s, matching every other repository in the codebase (none of which use `$transaction` for exactly this reason — now noted in a repository comment). Also found and repaired unrelated stale test-environment state while investigating (see credentials note above: a `PENDING_VERIFICATION` trainer, and `prompt10test`'s own lapsed subscription). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `workout.validators.spec.ts`, 29 tests, full API suite 126/126 green) all pass, plus live curl covering the inactive/wrong-role trainer rejections, plan duplicate (with exercises), assign→mark-progress→one-active-plan-per-member-supersede→remove, deleted-plan-cannot-assign, inactive-plan-cannot-assign, and progress-rejected-for-an-exercise-not-on-the-plan — plus a full Playwright E2E (exercise create/edit→plan create→weekly schedule add+persist-after-reload→assign from the plan page→duplicate from the list→member-detail Workout card shows the plan→mark progress→remove→dashboard/attendance/memberships/staff regression) — zero console errors.

18 — **Diet & Nutrition Management module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Diet & Nutrition Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `diet` feature): new `diet` module — same shape as Workout Management (Prompt 17), five new tables, all RLS'd via a hand-written migration (`20260723093500_enable_rls_diet_nutrition_management`, right after the model migration `20260723093411_diet_nutrition_management_module`): `Food` (the reusable food library — name/category/servingSize/calories/protein/carbohydrates/fat/fiber/sugar/sodium/notes/soft-delete, no image field this time — not in the spec's field list), `DietPlan` (name/description/goal/dailyCalories/durationDays/trainerId/soft-delete — `durationDays` rather than Workout's `durationWeeks`, a deliberate naming choice matching how diet plans are usually described, e.g. "30-day plan"), `DietPlanMeal` (the meal builder — one row per food placed under a `mealType` slot: BREAKFAST/MORNING_SNACK/LUNCH/EVENING_SNACK/DINNER/PRE_WORKOUT/POST_WORKOUT — **not** tied to a day-of-week like Workout's schedule, since a diet plan's meals repeat every day of the plan rather than following a weekly rotation; `sortOrder` is the Meal Builder's ordering within one meal type), `MemberDietPlan` (one row per assignment, same "supersede the old ACTIVE row to COMPLETED, never mutate" history pattern as `MemberWorkoutPlan`), `MemberDietDailyLog` (one row per assignment+calendar-date — "Daily Tracking"+"Water Intake"+"Weight Tracking" all in one row, `mealsStatus` kept as a small Json map rather than a normalized per-meal child table since a day only ever has ≤7 slots). New perms `diets:view/create/update/delete/restore/assign/progress` (distinct from the pre-existing speculative `diet-plans:manage`, left seeded but unused, same precedent as every prior module) — MANAGER gets all 7, TRAINER gets view/create/update/assign/progress (no delete/restore), RECEPTIONIST gets view only — identical grant shape to Workout Management. Business rule "only active trainers can create or assign diet plans" enforced via the same `assertTrainerActive()` pattern (copy of Workout's, not shared — see BACKEND-GUIDE.md for why sharing wasn't worth it here). Progress upserts MERGE against whatever was already logged for that date rather than overwriting (marking one meal complete must not wipe an already-logged water intake or a different meal's status logged in an earlier call that same day) — a deliberate design point absent from Workout's simpler per-exercise-row progress model. Frontend: Diet Plan List/Create/Edit (`/diet-plans`, `/diet-plans/new`, `/diet-plans/[planId]` — the edit page hosts the Meal Builder, no drag-and-drop this time since the prompt's Diet frontend feature list asked for "Meal Builder," not reordering), Food Library (`/diet-plans/foods`, Dialog-based CRUD, same pattern as Exercise Library), and a new "Diet" card on the Member detail page (current assignment + today's water/weight tracking + per-meal Done/Skip + Assign/Remove + collapsible history). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `diet.validators.spec.ts`, 26 tests, full API suite 152/152 green) all pass, plus live curl covering the wrong-role/inactive-trainer rejections, plan duplicate (with meals — the meal-builder persistence was double-checked via a fresh GET specifically because Workout's equivalent had silently failed until fixed), assign→merge-progress-across-three-separate-calls→one-active-plan-per-member-supersede→remove, deleted-plan-cannot-assign, inactive-plan-cannot-assign, and progress-rejected-for-a-meal-type-not-on-the-plan — plus a full Playwright E2E (food create/edit→plan create→meal builder add+persist-after-reload→assign from the plan page→duplicate from the list→member-detail Diet card shows the plan→save daily tracking→mark meal progress→remove→dashboard/workout-plans/attendance/memberships/staff regression) — zero console errors.

19 — **Finance Management module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Finance Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `finance` feature): new `modules/finance/` — four routers (`/payments`, `/invoices`, `/income`, `/expenses`, plus `/finance/summary` for the dashboard), six new tables all RLS'd via a hand-written migration (`20260725071300_enable_rls_finance_management`, right after the model migration `20260725071154_finance_management_module`). Models are deliberately named `MemberPayment`/`MemberInvoice`/`MemberInvoiceItem` — **not** `Payment`/`Invoice`, which already exist from Prompt 8 as FitCloud's own tenant-billing models (a hard Prisma name collision was avoided; these two concerns share no code). `MemberPaymentMethod` similarly avoids colliding with the pre-existing `PaymentMethod` model (a saved payment method for the tenant's OWN subscription). `MemberInvoice` can be created standalone ("Generate Invoice," status UNPAID) or auto-generated the moment a `MemberPayment` succeeds with no invoice already attached — the business rule "every successful membership payment automatically generates an invoice." A `MemberPaymentRefund` row is always a NEW row (never a mutation of the original payment) — "refunds must create financial history" — supporting multiple partial refunds before a payment is fully `REFUNDED`. An `Income` row tagged `MEMBERSHIP_FEE` is also auto-created on every successful payment (`sourcePaymentId` unique, one per payment, not editable/deletable directly) so the revenue ledger and payment history never drift apart; every other `Income`/`Expense` category is entered manually. New perms `finance:view/payment-create/payment-refund/invoice-view/invoice-download/income-manage/expense-manage` (distinct from the pre-existing speculative `payments:create/read/refund`, left seeded but unused, same precedent as every prior module) — MANAGER gets all 7, RECEPTIONIST gets view/payment-create/invoice-view/invoice-download (front-desk records payments and views/downloads invoices; refunds and income/expense ledger management stay Manager-only), TRAINER gets none (finance isn't part of a trainer's job in this design). **First real PDF generation in this codebase** — added `pdfkit` (pure-JS, no native binary) specifically because this module's spec explicitly asked for "Download Invoice (PDF)," unlike the Prompt-8 platform invoice module which deliberately shipped HTML-only ("no renderer existed at the time... swapping one in only touches this one method" — this prompt is that swap-in, for a *different* invoice model). Frontend: Payment List (`/payments`, also hosts the Finance Dashboard widgets: Today's/Monthly Income, Monthly Expenses, Outstanding Payments, a 30-day Revenue Trend chart) + Record Payment (`/payments/new`) + Payment Details (`/payments/[id]`, refund/cancel/verify actions); Invoice List (`/invoices`) + Generate Invoice (`/invoices/new`, dynamic line-item builder) + Invoice Details (`/invoices/[id]`, download PDF/print/email); Income List (`/income`, Dialog-based manual entry, same pattern as Exercise/Food Library) + Expense List (`/expenses`) + Create Expense (`/expenses/new`, dedicated page with receipt upload reusing the exact `MemberDocument` data-URL convention) — a deliberate asymmetry matching the prompt's own frontend page list (Income has no separate create page, Expense does). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `finance.validators.spec.ts`, 28 tests, full API suite 180/180 green) all pass, plus live curl covering the full payment→auto-invoice→auto-income chain, duplicate-payment-for-an-already-settled-invoice rejection, partial-then-full refund history, PDF download (confirmed a real binary via `file`, not HTML), a PENDING payment correctly skipping auto-invoice/income until it succeeds, auto-generated income entries correctly rejecting direct edits, and the dashboard summary's numbers cross-checked against the payments/income/expenses just created — plus a full Playwright E2E (record payment→verify status→auto-generated invoice linked→refund→generate manual invoice→download PDF→add manual income→create expense→dashboard/members/workout-plans/diet-plans/attendance regression) — zero console errors.

20 — **Reports & Analytics module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Reports & Analytics, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `reports` feature): new `modules/reports/` — four routers (`/reports/dashboard`, `/reports` — the 11 named reports + generic export, `/reports/scheduled`, `/analytics` — the 7 trend/comparison endpoints). Unlike every prior module, this one adds only ONE new table (`ScheduledReport`, RLS'd via `20260726102900_enable_rls_reports_analytics` right after the model migration `20260726102755_reports_analytics_module`) — everything else is read-only aggregation directly against the tables every prior module already built (Member, Attendance, Membership, MembershipPlan, MemberPayment, MemberInvoice, Income, Expense, WorkoutPlan, DietPlan, User/StaffProfile, Branch). Dashboard: `getKpis` (12 metrics — Total/Active/Inactive/New Members, Expiring Memberships, Today's Attendance, Monthly Revenue/Expenses, Outstanding Payments, Total Staff, Active Trainers, Active Branches) and `getRecentActivities` (a merged, sorted feed of recent payments/check-ins/new members) — both branch-scoped. The 11 reports (Membership/Attendance/Revenue/Expense/Payment/Staff/Trainer-Performance/Member-Progress/Branch-Performance/Expiring-Membership/Active-vs-Inactive) are either paginated lists or flat aggregate arrays depending on shape. The 7 analytics endpoints (Revenue/Attendance Trends, Membership/New-Member Growth, Retention, Payment Collection, Branch Comparison) all return a shared trend-point shape for charting, gap-filled via a newly-extracted `fillDailyTrend` util (generalizing the copy-pasted pattern from Attendance/Finance's own trend builders — first time it's been shared instead of re-copied). "Member Retention" is a **deliberate simplification**: a daily active-member-count snapshot, not full cohort analysis — documented as out of scope for this pass. New perms `reports:view`/`reports:export`/`analytics:view` (distinct from the pre-existing speculative `reports:read`/`reports:view-financial`, left seeded but unused, same precedent as every prior module) — MANAGER gets all 3, TRAINER and RECEPTIONIST get `reports:view` only (no export, no analytics). Branch scoping ("users can only access data for authorized branches") is centralized in one `resolveBranchScope()` util reused by every report/analytics method — verified live directly against a real single-branch-restricted manager account (their own branch succeeds, a different branch throws `ForbiddenError`, no filter scopes to their allowed set). Export (`GET /reports/:reportType/export?format=csv|xlsx|pdf`) is one generic dispatcher covering all 18 report/analytics types — reuses the established ExcelJS pattern (Attendance) and adds a second `pdfkit` use site (first was Finance's invoice PDF) for a generic tabular PDF renderer. Scheduled Reports ("generated automatically for future email delivery") is a real persisted `ScheduledReport` model (name/reportType/frequency DAILY-WEEKLY-MONTHLY/recipientEmails/branchId/nextRunAt) plus a genuine BullMQ repeatable job (`jobs/scheduled-reports.jobs.ts`, registered in `server.ts` exactly like `subscription-billing.jobs.ts` — hourly sweep, not a fake stub) that emails a plain-text CSV summary inline (no attachment support exists in the mail infra — same documented limitation as Finance's "Email Invoice"). A schedule's `branchId` is validated once against its creator's access at creation time, since the BullMQ sweep runs as a background system job with no "current user" to re-check against later. Frontend: the Prompt-10 dashboard foundation's three explicitly-documented placeholders (`StatsGrid`, `ChartsGrid`, `RecentActivity` — each code-commented "real values arrive once Members/Attendance/Payments exist") are now wired to real data from this module, exactly as originally planned; Reports Center (`/reports`, a card grid linking to a generic Report Viewer at `/reports/[reportType]` plus the Scheduled Reports management panel); Analytics Dashboard (`/analytics`, all 4 required chart types — Area for Revenue Trends/Payment Collection, Line for Attendance Trends/Retention, Bar for Membership/New-Member Growth, Pie for Branch Comparison revenue share). **Bug found + fixed during live testing**: `revenueReport`'s Prisma `where` clause included a stray `deletedAt: null` — `MemberPayment` has no such column. TypeScript's excess-property check didn't catch it because the `where` object was built as a separate `const` (not an inline literal) before being passed to `findMany`/`count`/`aggregate`, which bypasses that check entirely; Prisma's runtime validation correctly rejected the unknown field with a 500. Fixed by removing the incorrect field; every other `deletedAt` usage across the module was audited against its actual model afterward and confirmed correct. **Second bug found + fixed during the Playwright pass**: the generic Report Viewer's table used `row.memberCode` as the React list key, which collides whenever a report has multiple rows for the same member (e.g. Attendance — one row per visit) — fixed by keying on the full row content plus its array index instead of a single field. Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `reports.validators.spec.ts`, 18 tests, full API suite 198/198 green) all pass, plus live curl covering all 11 reports, all 7 analytics endpoints, dashboard KPIs/recent-activities, CSV/Excel/PDF export (Excel/PDF confirmed real binaries via `file`, not text), branch-scope denial (direct unit-level verification against a real restricted account), and full scheduled-report CRUD + run-now — plus a full Playwright E2E (dashboard KPIs/charts render→Reports Center→Report Viewer→Analytics Dashboard→create/run/delete a scheduled report→export CSV→dashboard/payments/members regression) — zero console errors after both fixes.

21 — **Notifications & Communication module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Notifications & Communication, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `notifications`/`announcements` features): extends the pre-existing Prompt-10 `tenant-notifications` module in place (list/mark-read/mark-all-read/`notifyTenant`/`broadcast` already existed) rather than replacing it — added `POST /notifications` (manual create, wiring an already-built-but-unexposed repository method), `GET /notifications/:id`, `DELETE /notifications/:id`, `GET /notifications/unread-count`, and retrofitted `requirePermission('notifications:view'|'notifications:manage')` onto every route (previously these three routes had no permission check at all — a real gap this prompt closed). `TenantNotificationCategory` enum extended (additive) with `MEMBER`/`MEMBERSHIP`/`PAYMENT`/`ATTENDANCE`/`WORKOUT`/`DIET`/`STAFF` alongside the existing `ANNOUNCEMENT`/`SYSTEM`/`SUBSCRIPTION`/`GENERAL`. New `NotificationTemplate` model (tenant-scoped, RLS'd) backs the 10 named templates (Membership Expiry/Payment Success/Payment Failed/Membership Renewal/New Member Registration/Attendance Confirmation/Workout Assignment/Diet Assignment/Welcome Message/Birthday Wishes) — a tenant only gets a DB row once it customizes a template; until then `NotificationTemplateService#getEffective` falls back to a hardcoded default map (`constants/default-templates.ts`), so no onboarding-time seeding step was needed. `TenantNotificationChannel` enum (IN_APP/EMAIL/PUSH/SMS/WHATSAPP — named to avoid colliding with the pre-existing platform-plane `NotificationChannel` enum, same collision-avoidance precedent as `TenantNotification` itself) — only IN_APP and EMAIL actually fire; PUSH/SMS are "(Future)"; WHATSAPP is a schema-only placeholder for the spec's "architecture should allow adding WhatsApp in the future" requirement. New `modules/tenant-notifications/services/notification-trigger.service.ts` exposes one function per trigger event, each rendering `{{placeholders}}` through the effective template and firing IN_APP (existing Notification Center) and/or EMAIL (direct to the member's own inbox — there's no member-facing in-app portal in this codebase) per the template's configured channels; two events (Membership Assigned, Staff Invitation) have no matching named template and fire a plain `notifyTenant` call instead. New **`modules/tenant-announcements/`** — a brand-new tenant-plane module for in-gym announcement authoring (Create/Update/Delete/Publish/Schedule/List), deliberately named `TenantAnnouncement`/`/tenant-announcements` to avoid colliding with the pre-existing PLATFORM-plane `Announcement` model/`/announcements` route (an admin-authored banner broadcast by subscription status — untouched, different concern entirely), same naming precedent as Finance's `MemberPayment` vs. `Payment`. `TenantAnnouncementStatus` (DRAFT→SCHEDULED|PUBLISHED→EXPIRED) with a genuine BullMQ hourly sweep (`jobs/tenant-announcement-scheduler.jobs.ts`, same shape as `subscription-billing.jobs.ts`/`scheduled-reports.jobs.ts`) that auto-publishes due `SCHEDULED` rows and auto-expires `PUBLISHED` rows past their own `expiresAt`; publishing (manual or scheduled) always also writes a `TenantNotification` (category `ANNOUNCEMENT`) with the HTML body stripped to plain text. A published/expired announcement can't be edited (`ConflictError`) — delete and recreate instead. New **membership-expiry sweep** (`modules/members/jobs/membership-expiry.jobs.ts`) — the first thing in this codebase that actually closes the loop on `Membership.status` never auto-transitioning off `ACTIVE` when `endDate` passes (previously only explicit cancel/renew/upgrade actions changed it; Reports' Expiring/Active-vs-Inactive reports query `endDate` directly, not status). Daily sweep: reminds within a 7-day window (Redis-deduped so it fires once per membership) and flips truly-expired `ACTIVE` memberships to `EXPIRED`, firing Membership Expiring/Expired respectively. New trigger wiring (all net-new call sites — none of these six modules called into notifications before this prompt): Member creation (New Member Registration + Welcome Message email), Membership assign/renew, Payment success/failure (`onPaymentSucceeded` — the same centralized hook Finance already had for auto-invoice/auto-income), Workout/Diet plan assignment, Attendance check-in/check-out (in addition to, not replacing, the pre-existing direct `attendance:checkin`/`attendance:checkout` socket events used for cache invalidation), and Staff invitation. New perms `notifications:view` (new — `notifications:manage` already existed in the catalog, previously unused/unenforced), `announcements:view/create/update/delete/publish` — MANAGER gets all of them, TRAINER and RECEPTIONIST get `notifications:view`+`announcements:view` only (read-only), matching every prior module's staff-role tightening pattern. Frontend: the Notification Center drawer and the `/notifications` History page (previously two near-duplicate copies of the same tab/list markup, flagged as a TODO in FRONTEND-GUIDE.md) were finally consolidated into one shared `NotificationFeed` component now that 7 more categories and a Delete action needed adding — a third copy would have made the divergence worse. History page gained real pagination (page/limit/totalPages from the API, replacing a fixed `limit:50`), a client-side search box, and delete. New `/notifications/settings` page manages the 10 templates (channels/title/body/active, via a Dialog). New `/announcements` page (Announcement Management) — status-tab list, a rich-text `Dialog` editor (bold/italic/underline/bullet-list/link via `document.execCommand` on a `contentEditable` div — no new npm dependency, same "reach for the native browser API first" precedent as Workout's drag-and-drop schedule editor), Publish-now and Schedule (a second small Dialog with a `datetime-local` input) actions, delete via the shared `ConfirmDialog`. New nav item "Announcements" (`icon: Megaphone`, `permission: 'announcements:view'`, `featureFlag: 'notifications'`) — the pre-existing "Notifications" nav item was left exactly as-is (still ungated) to avoid changing existing behavior. **Real bug found + fixed during the Playwright pass**: the new Notification Settings page nested a `Badge` (renders a `<div>`) inside a `<p>` for the channel/active/customized tag row — invalid HTML, a hydration error — same class of mistake as Prompt 16's dashboard tip and Prompt 15's Checkbox-`id` gap; fixed by changing the wrapper to a `<div>`. Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `tenant-announcement.validators.spec.ts` + `tenant-notification.validators.spec.ts`, 22 tests, full API suite 220/220 green) all pass, plus live curl covering create/read/delete/unread-count, template list+customize+re-fetch-reflects-override, the full announcement lifecycle (create→edit-while-draft→publish→edit-rejected-409→notification-center-entry-confirmed, plus a second schedule→reject-past-date→delete), all 12 trigger events fired end-to-end against real actions with a real Mailpit welcome email confirmed, and both new BullMQ queues confirmed registered in Redis (`bull:membership-expiry:*`, `bull:tenant-announcement-scheduler:*`) — plus a full Playwright E2E (bell drawer→History page+search→Notification Settings→edit a template→Announcements page→create draft→publish→confirm it appears in the Notification Center→delete→dashboard/members/reports regression) — zero console errors after the hydration fix.

22 — **Branch Management module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Branch Management, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md `branch` feature): expands `modules/branches/` from Prompt 10's read-only scaffold (`GET /branches`, name/isDefault/isActive/timezone only) into full CRUD — additive schema migration, no RLS migration needed since `branches` already had RLS enabled from its original creation. `Branch` gained branchCode (auto-gen, e.g. "BR-0001"), email/phone/whatsappNumber, addressLine1-2/city/state/country/postalCode, latitude/longitude, `operatingHours` (Json, same `{open,close,closed}`-per-weekday shape as `TenantSettings.businessHours` from Prompt 12, one level down at the branch), `holidays` (a lightweight Json array of `{date,label}` — never queried/filtered, so no child table), capacity/maxMembers/maxStaff, allowCheckIn, notes, and `deletedAt` (soft-delete — didn't exist before). Two new `@@unique([tenantId, ...])` constraints (branchCode, name) — since every one of the 27 pre-existing tenants had exactly one branch, the migration added `branch_code` nullable, backfilled every existing row to `"BR-0001"`, then set it `NOT NULL`, since `prisma migrate dev` can't add a required column to a non-empty table non-interactively. **Critical compatibility constraint**: `GET /branches` was, and is used by, the portal's branch selector and every `BranchSelect` dropdown across the entire app (Members/Attendance/Workout/Diet/Finance forms) as a plain unfiltered active-branch array — repurposing that exact endpoint into the new paginated management list would have broken every one of those consumers. Fixed by adding `GET /branches/assignable` (same naming/behavior precedent as `membership-plans`/`workout-plans`/`diet-plans`'s own `/assignable` endpoints) that preserves the original plain-array response exactly, and repointing the frontend's `branchService.list()` to that new path — every existing consumer (`useBranches`/`useCurrentBranch`, the header's branch selector, every `BranchSelect`) needed zero changes beyond that one URL. Business rules in `BranchService`: unique name/code (app-level pre-check + the DB constraint as a backstop), `assertBranchCapacity()` against `TenantLimit.maxBranches` (mirrors Staff/Member's capacity-check pattern — first time it's enforced for branches), the default branch can't be deactivated or deleted, a tenant must always keep ≥1 active branch (checked independently on both deactivate and delete), `setDefault` requires the target branch to already be active and clears every other branch's flag first (sequential, not a batched `$transaction`, per this codebase's tenant-scoped-client rule), and restore brings a branch back both un-deleted AND active in one step (same "restore = fully usable again" precedent as Membership Plans/Staff/Members). New perms `branches:view/create/update/delete/restore/activate` (distinct from the pre-existing speculative `branches:read`/`branches:manage`, left seeded but unused, same precedent as every prior module) — MANAGER gets all 6; TRAINER and RECEPTIONIST get `branches:view` only, closing a real pre-existing gap (neither role had ever been granted `branches:read`, meaning the branch selector's underlying query may have been silently 403-ing for those roles since Prompt 10 — this prompt is what finally fixes that, matching the `:view` grant every other module gives its non-Manager staff roles). Frontend: Branch List (`/branches` — search/sort/pagination/status-filter/set-default/activate-deactivate/delete/restore, replacing the old "coming soon" placeholder), Create Branch (`/branches/new`) and Branch Detail/Edit (`/branches/[branchId]`, one-big-form + `UnsavedChangesBar`, same shape as every other detail page in this app) sharing one `BranchFormFields` component across Create and Edit. Operating Hours reuses the **Gym Settings module's** `BusinessHoursEditor` component directly (cross-feature reuse — identical `{open,close,closed}`-per-weekday shape, first time a Prompt-12 component has been reused by a later module). Holidays get a small new `HolidaysEditor` (add/remove date+label list, no drag-and-drop needed). Sidebar "Branches" (pre-existing nav item, permission bumped from the old speculative `branches:read` to `branches:view`, same pattern as every prior module). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (new `branch.validators.spec.ts`, 10 tests, full API suite 230/230 green) all pass, plus live curl covering full CRUD, duplicate name/code rejection, default-branch deactivate/delete protection, the last-active-branch protection, set-default correctly moving the flag, soft-delete/restore, the plan's `maxBranches` limit correctly rejecting a 4th branch, and tenant isolation (a real cross-tenant branch ID 404s rather than leaking data) — plus a full Playwright E2E (branch list + search→view/edit an existing branch via the UnsavedChangesBar→attempt Create Branch at the plan's branch limit and confirm the error surfaces correctly→dashboard/header-branch-selector/members/attendance regression) — zero unexpected console errors (the one 409 logged is the intentional plan-limit test, same "expected error from an intentional negative test" precedent as Attendance's duplicate-check-in test).

23 — **Global Loading & Performance Optimization module** (see BACKEND-GUIDE.md §Global Loading & Performance Optimization, FRONTEND-GUIDE.md's loading/performance patterns): an app-wide UX/infra pass, not a new business feature — explicitly scoped to add no business-logic changes. Discovered significant pre-existing infrastructure to extend rather than rebuild: a `TopProgressBar` + Redux `ui-slice.pendingRequests` counter already existed (driven by the axios client's request/response interceptors), the axios client already had **request de-duplication** (an identical in-flight request is aborted in favor of the newest one) and full token-refresh handling, and a `LoadingButton` component already existed — just scoped to `features/auth/components/`, used only by auth/billing/onboarding forms. This prompt's job was mostly promotion, extension, and closing real gaps rather than building from scratch. **Frontend — Global Page Loader & Navigation Loader**: new `useDelayedLoading(isLoading, {showDelayMs, minDurationMs})` hook (a 100-150ms show-delay swallows near-instant operations entirely; a 250-300ms minimum display time once shown prevents an abrupt flash-then-gone) is now the anti-flicker primitive behind `TopProgressBar` itself. New `ui-slice.navigationPending` + `NavigationProgressProvider` (patches `history.pushState`/`replaceState`/`popstate` once at the module level — how the Next.js App Router performs client-side navigation under the hood, so every trigger the spec lists — sidebar, header menu, breadcrumb, tabs, internal links, browser back/forward — is covered without instrumenting each one) feeds a second signal into the same `TopProgressBar`, which now also animates to 100% and fades on completion instead of jumping away mid-progress. New `InitialLoadOverlay` (a plain `'use client'` component rendered as a sibling of `AppProviders` directly in the root layout, so it's present in the very first server-rendered HTML with zero provider dependencies) covers the initial-app-load case, fading out after hydration. **Bug found + fixed during live Playwright testing**: the history-patch's `dispatch(navigationStarted())` call fired synchronously inside `pushState`/`replaceState`, which the Next.js App Router itself sometimes calls from `useInsertionEffect`-timed internals — React forbids scheduling updates from there, surfacing as a real `useInsertionEffect must not schedule updates` console error on every login→dashboard navigation. Fixed by deferring the dispatch through `queueMicrotask()`. **Button loading state**: `LoadingButton` promoted from `features/auth/components/` to the shared `components/ui/` kit (18 existing auth/billing/onboarding import sites repointed, zero behavior change) and established as the standard going forward — applied directly to 9 "Create X" pages (Branches/Diet Plans/Expenses/Invoices/Memberships/Payments/Staff/Users/Workout Plans) and, at much higher leverage, to two shared components used across the whole app: `UnsavedChangesBar`'s Save button (used by 10 detail/edit pages — Members/Staff/Branches/Membership Plans/Workout Plans/Diet Plans/all 4 Gym Settings pages) and `ConfirmDialog`'s Confirm button (used by 20 delete/activate/deactivate/restore/bulk-action call sites app-wide). Also closed a real gap: Staff's and Users' CSV Export buttons had **no loading feedback at all** (a plain click handler, no spinner, no disabled state) — fixed via the new `useSubmitHandler` hook. **Form submission**: new `useSubmitHandler(action)` hook — a `ref`-based guard that blocks a duplicate concurrent submit even in the render-frame window before a disabled button visually stops accepting clicks (closing the one gap `mutation.isPending` alone can't cover), plus centralized error-to-message normalization; applied to the Contact Sales/Billing dialog (previously hand-rolled try/catch/setSubmitting boilerplate) and both CSV Export buttons. Documented as available for forms that don't already go through a TanStack `useMutation` (which gives `isPending` for free). **Table loading**: `DataTable` extended with a genuine error/retry state (`error`/`onRetry` props — previously a failed query had no distinct rendering path and silently fell through to the empty state) and applied across all 17 pages using it; its inline skeleton extracted into a standalone reusable `TableSkeleton`. New component library: `FullScreenLoader`, `PageLoader`, `SectionLoader`, `CardSkeleton`, `FormSkeleton` (Button/Inline Spinner/Progress Bar/Table Skeleton already existed and were reused, not recreated). **API request optimization**: axios client gained automatic retry with exponential backoff for network failures/timeouts/5xx — **GET requests only**, deliberately never for POST/PATCH/PUT/DELETE (auto-retrying a mutation risks a duplicate side effect, e.g. double-creating a record, if the original request actually reached the server and only the response was lost); a distinct "Request timed out" message for `ECONNABORTED`. Debounced search: the pre-existing `useDebouncedValue` hook (previously buried in `features/onboarding/hooks/`, its only consumer) was promoted to a shared `src/hooks/` location and applied to all 14 list pages with a live search box (Members/Staff/Branches/Workout Plans/Diet Plans/Exercises/Foods/Payments/Invoices/Income/Expenses/Attendance History/Memberships/Users) — previously every keystroke changed the query key and triggered an immediate refetch. **Data caching**: per-hook `staleTime` overrides for the spec's named cache list — Dashboard/Staff/Members/Membership-Plans-list at 1 minute, Branches/Membership-Plans-assignable/all 6 Gym Settings queries at 5 minutes (Permissions Registry already had a 5-minute staleTime from Prompt 11; User Profile is sourced from Redux + redux-persist, not a TanStack query at all, so it's already effectively cached for the whole session). Every mutation's existing `invalidateQueries` call still forces an immediate refetch after a real change — cache invalidation-on-update was already a fully established pattern, not something this prompt needed to add. **Performance**: `next/dynamic` applied to the camera-based QR Scanner (attendance check-in) and the Recharts-heavy dashboard `ChartsGrid` (both `ssr:false`, with a skeleton/section-loader fallback); `next.config.mjs` gained `experimental.optimizePackageImports` for `lucide-react`/`recharts` (tree-shakes both barrel-file libraries, used on nearly every page); `StatisticCard` (rendered many times per KPI grid) wrapped in `React.memo`; new `@tanstack/react-virtual` dependency backs a generic `VirtualizedList` component plus genuine row-virtualization applied to the Permission Matrix (the one place in the app with a real unpaginated, potentially-100+-row client-rendered table — confirmed live at only ~24 `<tr>` mounted instead of the full catalog). Every other list in the app already paginates server-side (20/page), which is the established, correct fix for "large datasets" — virtualization was reserved for the one place that genuinely needed it rather than applied indiscriminately. **Backend**: new `requestTimeoutMiddleware` (hand-rolled, no new dependency) — a 30-second safety-net ceiling independent of the client's own 15s axios timeout, mounted early in `app.ts`; new `RequestTimeoutError`/`REQUEST_TIMEOUT` error code (additive to the stable error-code contract). `compression()` and per-tenant pagination were already fully in place from earlier prompts — confirmed, not re-added. Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (full API suite 230/230 green, no backend business logic touched) all pass, plus a full Playwright pass covering navigation (sidebar/back/forward)/debounced search (confirmed via a request-count listener)/the virtualized Permission Matrix/a LoadingButton-driven Create flow/dynamic-imported components/regression across Members/Staff/Reports/Notifications/Announcements — zero unexpected console errors after fixing the `useInsertionEffect` bug found during this same pass.

24 — **Header branch-switcher wiring (bug fix, not a new module)**: the header's `BranchSelector` (`components/layout/branch-selector.tsx`) dispatched `branchSelected(id)` into the persisted `branch` Redux slice, but a repo-wide check found **nothing else read that value** — every backend endpoint it should have fed (Dashboard KPIs/charts, Members list, Staff list) already accepted and correctly filtered by a `branchId` query param (`resolveBranchScope` in `reports/services/dashboard.service.ts`, plain `where.branchId`/`where.userBranches` checks in `members`/`staff` repositories) — this was a pure frontend wiring gap, confirmed by grepping for `useCurrentBranch`/`currentBranchId` usage (3 files total, none of them a page). Fixed by passing `useCurrentBranch().currentBranchId` into `useKpis`/`useAttendanceTrends`/`useRevenueTrends`/`useNewMemberGrowth`/`useRecentActivities` (dashboard), `useMemberList` (`/members`), `useStaffList` (`/staff`), and `useUsers` (`/users` — "Staff & Access", the generic IAM module, found broken separately by live user testing after the first fix landed) — all four already had the `branchId` param threaded through their hook signatures and TanStack query keys, just never called with a value. The IAM `/users` list's backend filter (`user-management.repository.ts`) is `OR: [{allBranches: true}, {userBranches: {some: {branchId}}}]` — a user with `allBranches: true` (e.g. Owner, or any IAM user without branch restriction) still shows under every branch, which is the correct semantic and worth matching if any other module grows a similar filter. Also: Attendance dashboard/history, the Analytics Dashboard, and the generic Report Viewer (`reports/[reportType]`, same `ReportFiltersBar` component as Analytics) each already had their own **independent** local branch-filter dropdown (defaulting to "all branches" every page visit, with zero relationship to the header) — these four now seed from, and re-sync to, `currentBranchId` via a `useEffect`, while remaining locally overridable within that page visit (needed for e.g. Analytics' branch-comparison chart, which is only useful across all branches).

25 — **Razorpay Online Payments (Payment Links) for Member Payments**: real Razorpay integration added to the Prompt-19 `modules/finance/` Member Payments flow — a member/staff-facing "gym receives payment" surface, deliberately distinct from Prompt 8's platform Billing gateway (which is a sandboxed Stripe/Razorpay/PayPal stub charging TENANTS for their own FitCloud subscription — untouched, no shared code). New `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID`/`RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET` env vars (`config/env.ts`'s `env.razorpay.{keyId,keySecret,isConfigured}` getter, same pattern as `env.pusher`) — dev `.env` carries real Razorpay **test-mode** credentials (`rzp_test_...`); go-live only requires swapping in live keys under the same var names, no code change. New `services/razorpay-gateway.service.ts` wraps the `razorpay` npm SDK's **Payment Links API** (`paymentLink.create`/`paymentLink.fetch`) — deliberately NOT the Orders API + client-side Checkout widget, because of an explicit correction mid-build: **staff must never see or handle a card-entry UI**; the only two ways a gym-staff user can collect an online payment are (1) generate a Payment Link and send it to the member, who pays on Razorpay's own hosted page, or (2) mark it paid manually (the pre-existing offline flow — payment date/paid amount/due amount — completely unchanged). `POST /payments/razorpay/link` (`MemberPaymentService#createPaymentLink`) creates the `MemberPayment` row as `PENDING` (`method: ONLINE_GATEWAY`) plus a real Razorpay Payment Link, returning `{payment, shortUrl, paymentLinkId}`; requires the member to have an email or phone on file (`ValidationError` otherwise — Razorpay needs one to notify them). No public webhook URL exists in local dev, so confirmation is a manual poll: the pre-existing `POST /payments/:id/verify` stub (previously just an authoritative read of the stored status, permission bumped `finance:view`→`finance:payment-create` since it can now mutate) was extended so that for a PENDING `ONLINE_GATEWAY` payment it calls `fetchPaymentLink` and flips the payment to `SUCCESS` (reusing the same `onPaymentSucceeded` hook Prompt 19 built — auto-invoice + auto-income + notification, no duplicate logic) or `FAILED` (expired/cancelled link) accordingly; still PENDING just returns PENDING. Frontend: `/payments/new`'s Online channel (renamed "Send Payment Link", `Link2` icon, no card icon anywhere) collects amount/discount/tax/notes same as before, then on submit shows a read-only short URL + Copy button + a "Check payment status" button (`useVerifyPaymentStatus`, now invalidates payments/invoices/finance on success since it can mutate) instead of ever opening a payment widget. Offline channel, the outstanding-invoice picker, and the paid/due-amount computation (still entirely client-side from `MemberInvoiceDetail.payments[]`, no new DTO fields) are unchanged from Prompt 19. Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/build(both apps)/vitest (finance validators suite now 31 tests — the 5 Order/Checkout-signature tests were removed since that flow no longer exists; full API suite 233/233 green) all pass, plus live curl against the real Razorpay test-mode API on the running dev stack: created a genuine Payment Link for `dana.lee`'s tenant against member Jamie Rivera (`MEM-0001`) and got back a real `https://rzp.io/...` short URL and `plink_...` ID, confirmed `/verify` genuinely polls Razorpay and returns `PENDING` for an unpaid link, confirmed the old `/razorpay/order` and `/razorpay/verify` routes are gone (404, and the latter now falls through to the generic `/:id/verify` pattern instead), then cancelled the test payment record afterward. **Not done this pass**: an interactive Playwright click-through of the `/payments/new` UI — no browser-automation tool was available in this session, so the frontend was only confirmed via typecheck/lint/build and a plain HTTP 200 on the page route, not an actual button click; the online/offline toggle and Copy/Check-status buttons should get a real Playwright pass next time this module is touched.

25.5 — **Razorpay notify channels + resend + status tracking (same-session follow-up)**: closed a gap in Prompt 25's first pass — Payment Links were always auto-notified on whatever contact details happened to exist, with no staff control and no visible way to resend or see the tracked status past a toast. `createPaymentLinkSchema`/`CreatePaymentLinkInput` gained optional `notifyEmail`/`notifySms` booleans (default to `Boolean(member.email)`/`Boolean(member.phone)` when omitted); `MemberPaymentService#createPaymentLink` now rejects requesting a channel the member has no matching contact detail for (`ValidationError`, e.g. "This member has no phone on file — uncheck..."), and `razorpay-gateway.service.ts#createPaymentLink` passes these through as Razorpay's own `notify.email`/`notify.sms` instead of always auto-deriving them. `PaymentLinkDto` now echoes back `notifiedEmail`/`notifiedSms` so the frontend can show what actually went out. New `POST /payments/:id/razorpay/notify` (`{ medium: 'email'|'sms' }`) wraps Razorpay's own `paymentLink.notifyBy` — a staff-triggered resend (e.g. "member says they never got the SMS") that doesn't cancel/recreate the link; validated the same way as verify (payment must be a PENDING `ONLINE_GATEWAY` payment with a stored link id). Frontend: `/payments/new`'s Online form gained two checkboxes ("Notify the member" — Email/SMS, each disabled when the member lacks that contact detail, defaulting to checked when available), and the post-generation panel gained "Resend by email"/"Resend by SMS" buttons (each independently loading-tracked via its own `useResendPaymentLinkNotification()` instance) plus a status `Badge` ("Awaiting payment"/"Paid"/"Failed / expired") that updates from the existing "Check payment status" click instead of only a toast. `useCreatePaymentLink` now also invalidates the `payments` query key on success (previously didn't, even though it creates a real PENDING row). Verified: typecheck/eslint(0 warnings, both apps)/vitest (finance validators suite now 35 tests, +6 for notify/resend; full API suite 237/237 green) all pass, plus live curl against Razorpay's real test-mode API — created a link with `notifyEmail:true, notifySms:false` and got back matching `notifiedEmail`/`notifiedSms` flags, resent it successfully by both email and SMS via the new endpoint, confirmed an invalid `medium` is rejected (422), and confirmed a member missing a requested contact channel is rejected with a clear message — test payment cancelled afterward. **Incident during this pass**: running `next build` while the tenant-web `next dev` server was live corrupted `.next` and killed the dev server mid-build (the exact documented gotcha above) — the dev server auto-restarted on its own but was killed-and-restarted once more deliberately to get a clean build; confirmed healthy (200) afterward. Given live ESTABLISHED connections to port 3001 during this pass (someone actively using the dev server), treat `next build` as genuinely disruptive, not just "documented as a gotcha" — ask before running it if the dev server might be in active use, same as any other action with real user-facing impact.

26 — **Membership Plans branch scoping + Finance pages branch wiring + app-wide validation-error display fix**: found live by the user (a plan created while "kaushalGym"'s default branch was selected still showed up under a different branch, "Kishor Akalya") — `listMembershipPlansQuerySchema` had no `branchId` filter at all (unlike Members/Staff/Users in Prompt 24, this wasn't a frontend-only wiring gap, the backend genuinely didn't support it). Added `branchId` end-to-end: query schema, `MembershipPlanRepository#buildWhere` (`OR: [{gymAccessAllBranches: true}, {accessBranchIds: {array_contains: branchId}}]` — a plan is visible at a branch if it's flagged for every branch OR that branch is in its JSON `accessBranchIds` list), `/memberships` page now passes `currentBranchId` and shows a new "Branches" column ("All" vs. the actual branch names) so the restriction is visible at a glance. Also wired the same header-branch pattern into `/payments`, `/invoices`, `/income`, `/expenses` (all four already had full `branchId` support end-to-end in `features/finance` — same "backend ready, frontend never called it" pattern as Prompt 24) — verified live: Main Branch vs. Downtown Branch shows different row counts on all four.

**Also found and fixed while investigating a "Request validation failed" report (Create Membership Plan)**: the API's `validate()` middleware already captured per-field zod errors in the response body (`errors: [{field, message}]`) — the bug was entirely client-side: `toAuthServiceError` (`features/auth/services/api-client.ts`, the ONE normalization function every feature's `toXError` wrapper funnels through) only read the top-level `message` ("Request validation failed") and discarded `errors[]`. Now builds a specific message from the field-level errors (e.g. "Validity End: Validity start date must be on or before the validity end date") when present, falling back to the old generic message otherwise — this fixes error display app-wide (Staff/Members/Branches/Roles/Memberships/etc.) from one shared location, no per-page changes needed. Root cause of the specific failure: `dateOfBirth`/`joiningDate` (Members + Staff) and `validityStart`/`validityEnd` (Membership Plans) were validated with zod's `.datetime()` (requires a full ISO timestamp) while every paired frontend control is a plain `<input type="date">` (produces `YYYY-MM-DD`) — swapped all six to `.date()`. Also added a missing cross-field rule (`validityStart <= validityEnd`) to the plan create/update schemas — previously any date ordering silently passed. Checked every other `.datetime()` field in the API (audit-logs, attendance check-in/out, users list-filter dates) — all either genuinely need time precision or have no live frontend consumer yet, left as-is. Verified: typecheck/eslint (0 warnings, both apps), full API vitest suite (233/233 green — one transient failure mid-session traced to the register endpoint's own rate limiter, `rl:register:*` in Redis, exhausted by repeated test/curl runs this session, confirmed innocent by re-running after its TTL expired), and live end-to-end via curl (backend-only reproduction of the exact reported bug, and its fix) plus a headless-Chromium (Playwright) script against the running dev stack: logged in as `dana.lee`, confirmed the exact screenshot reproduction (end-before-start dates → specific field error) and a corrected submission succeeding, and confirmed Payments/Invoices/Income/Expenses/Memberships all re-filter correctly on a live branch switch — zero console errors throughout. Also found the `tenant-web` dev server itself had ballooned past 2GB RSS mid-session (global 500s on every route, unrelated to any code change) — restarted cleanly, a process-health issue worth knowing about on long-running dev sessions, not a bug in this codebase.

26.5 — **Platform Billing: Razorpay-only checkout + INR pricing**: user-requested scope, explicitly limited to the platform Billing/subscription flow (Prompt 8 — FitCloud charging the TENANT for its own plan; NOT the Finance module's Member Payments, which already got its own Razorpay Payment Links integration in Prompt 25). `PAYMENT_PROVIDERS` (`features/onboarding/constants/index.ts`) trimmed from three entries (Stripe/Razorpay/PayPal) down to just Razorpay — this single array is `.map()`'d by both `features/billing/components/checkout-dialog.tsx` (in-app plan switch) and `features/onboarding/components/steps/payment-step.tsx` (wizard step 5), so trimming it fixed both UIs with no other component changes needed beyond updating each file's `useState<PaymentProvider>('stripe')` default to `'razorpay'`. Backend `provider` enum validators (`subscription.validators.ts`, `onboarding.validators.ts`) still accept all three values — deliberately left as-is, this was a UI-only restriction, not a backend lockdown. Currency: `SubscriptionPlan.currency` (Prisma, `@default("USD")`) is the single source of truth every billing screen's `formatMoney`/`Intl.NumberFormat` call already read from (`checkout-dialog.tsx`, `payment-step.tsx`, `plan-comparison.tsx`, `/billing/history`, `/billing/invoices` — no hardcoded `$` anywhere) — flipped to `currency: 'INR'` for all three seeded plans (`prisma/seed.ts`'s `PlanSeed`/`SUBSCRIPTION_PLANS`, both the `create` and `update` branches of the upsert) and re-ran the seed live against the dev DB (`npx prisma db seed`) to update the existing rows, confirmed via direct SQL. Prices were also rescaled to realistic INR amounts (not just relabeled) per explicit user choice: Starter ₹2,499/₹24,990 (was $29/$290), Professional ₹6,999/₹69,990 (was $79/$790), Enterprise ₹17,999/₹179,990 (was $199/$1,990) — same monthly→yearly ratio (~10×, i.e. 2 months free) preserved. Historical invoices/payments already issued under the old USD plan keep their own stored `currency`/amount unchanged (correct — invoices are financial history, not live-recomputed) — only the plan catalog and any NEW checkout going forward is INR. Also changed the hardcoded `'en-US'` locale in `subscription/events/billing-email.listeners.ts`'s invoice-total email formatting to `'en-IN'` (was mismatched with the now-INR currency it formats) and the super-admin Plan create-form's default `currency` (`apps/super-admin/.../plans/page.tsx`) from `'USD'` to `'INR'` so a platform admin creating a new plan defaults correctly. Verified: typecheck/eslint (0 warnings, api/tenant-web/super-admin), full API vitest suite 237/237 green, and live curl confirming `GET /onboarding/plans` now returns `currency: "INR"` with the new rescaled prices for all three plans. **Not done this pass**: an interactive browser check of the checkout dialog/wizard step actually rendering `₹` and a single Razorpay button — typecheck/lint only, no Playwright (same caveat as Prompt 25); also didn't re-run `next build` a second time in this same session given the earlier build-vs-live-dev-server incident (see Prompt 25.5) — rely on typecheck+lint as sufficient for this small, mechanical a change.

27 — **Complete UI/UX Redesign** (`apps/tenant-web` only, visual/CSS/markup changes exclusively — zero business-logic/API/routing/permission changes, see FRONTEND-GUIDE.md for the full pattern reference): a premium-SaaS visual overhaul (Linear/Stripe/Vercel/Clerk/Supabase-grade) applied in ordered phases across the entire tenant portal. **Design tokens** (`globals.css`, Tailwind v4 `@theme inline`): new soft layered elevation scale (`--shadow-xs/sm/md/lg/xl`, separately tuned per light/dark — dark needed more opacity, not just the same shadow on a dark bg), `--radius` bumped 0.625rem→0.75rem, Geist Sans/Mono self-hosted via the `geist` npm package (replacing the old system-font-only stack, wired in `app/layout.tsx` via `GeistSans.variable`/`GeistMono.variable` on `<html>`, the existing per-tenant branding inline `style` on `<body>` untouched), `.gradient-brand`/`.gradient-brand-radial`/`.text-gradient-brand`/`.glass-panel` utility classes built from `color-mix(in oklch, var(--primary) …)` so brand-derived gradients/glass stay tenant-reactive (never a hardcoded second brand hex), and a categorical chart palette `--chart-1..8` (slot 1 = live `var(--primary)`, slots 2-8 a fixed CVD-validated hex order — validated via the dataviz skill's `validate_palette.js` against this app's actual card surfaces, both light `#ffffff` and dark `#12131a` computed from the real OKLCH tokens — never reorder these slots or reuse `--success`/`--warning`/`--destructive` for a categorical series). **UI kit** (`components/ui/`): all ~32 existing primitives restyled in place with identical prop contracts (button gained `success`/`warning` variants; card's `CardTitle` fixed from a semantically-wrong `<h1>` to `<h3>`; badge's destructive/success/warning variants now soft-tint rather than solid; label gained an optional `required` boolean prop for the required-field asterisk;
