  - **Exercise library / detail / add** (frames 5, 5a, 5b) and **Food library / detail / add** (frames 7, 7a, 7b): full CRUD. Trainer has no `workouts:delete`/`diets:delete` grant, so no delete affordance is offered.
  - **Workout builder** (frame 6) + **Day editor** (frame 6a): 7-day grid, per-day exercise list. **Load-bearing detail**: `PATCH /workout-plans/:id/exercises` replaces the plan's *entire* weekly schedule, so the Day editor re-sends every other day's exercises unchanged alongside the edited day — same for `PATCH /diet-plans/:id/meals`.
  - **Meal builder** (frame 8) + **Add to meal** (frame 8a): Breakfast/Lunch/Dinner only (backend `MealType` has 7 values; the design shows exactly these 3). Unlike the workout builder, frame 8 has **no** "Assign to client" card — "Save plan" is its only CTA — so here Save both persists the meals and assigns the plan to the client whose flow opened the screen.
  - **Client progress** (frame 9) + **Workout log** (frame 9a): ring/percent/day-list from `GET /workout-plans/members/:memberId`; tap an exercise to cycle PENDING→COMPLETED→SKIPPED (`POST /workout-plans/assignments/:id/progress`), plus a trainer note (`PATCH /workout-plans/assignments/:id`). Two design details dropped as unbacked: the "@ 62kg" load (the API tracks status per exercise, never a weight) and the "On track" pill (shows the assignment's real status instead of a judgement the API doesn't make). The "· Week 3" subtitle **is** real — derived from the assignment's `startDate`. Sets×reps on the log come from a second `GET /workout-plans/:id` fetch, since progress entries carry no sets/reps.
  - **Trainer menu** (frame 4b): My work→Reports (reuses the Receptionist reports hub — permission-gated, not role-locked) + Support/Notifications. No Money/Programs sections, per the design.

  Verified: `flutter analyze` clean; a 26-endpoint live curl smoke test against the running stack with a **real TRAINER token**, all green (covering every endpoint the module calls, incl. create/update exercise+food, create plan, set-exercises, set-meals, assign, mark-progress, trainer-remarks); on-device via `uiautomator dump` with the same touch-lag workarounds as Chunks 3/5. Device-verified end to end: login→My clients (real roster), Assign→plan picker→builder→**"Assign plan" created a real ACTIVE assignment server-side**, Client progress (both the populated ring/day-list *and* the no-plan empty state), and Workout log where a single tap flipped an exercise PENDING→COMPLETED with a fresh `markedAt` confirmed via curl. This device would not accept synthetic taps on the bottom nav (mid-screen taps worked fine; the nav is unmodified shared chrome already proven in three other shells), so the Exercise/Food library tabs were verified by temporarily booting the shell on each tab index, then reverting. **Not yet built at the time**: Member entry point (Chunk 6) — landed in Prompt 56. The Chunk-2e design-fidelity gap was closed in Prompt 57.

56 — **Mobile app — Member module complete (Chunk 6; the mobile rebuild's last role)** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter). `MemberShell` — the design's 5-tab nav (Home/Workout/Diet/Classes/Menu) in the member palette (lime→teal), the only non-staff-accented shell; selected in `HomeRouterScreen` off `SessionAuthenticatedMember` (member auth itself shipped in Chunk 1 and was left landing on the stub until now). New `MemberPortalRepository` + models (`MemberPortalProfile`, `MemberVisit`, `MemberWorkoutAssignment`, `MemberDietAssignment`, `MemberBooking`); `MemberInvoice` and `ClassSession` reused unmodified from Chunk 5.
  - **The member plane's entire API surface is `/portal/*`** (one `memberAuthenticateMiddleware` gate at router level, no RBAC — every route is hard-scoped to the caller's own id server-side, so no repository method takes a memberId). Verified by grep: no other module mounts member auth.
  - **Design frame "4a. Renew" is NOT built, and the dashboard's "Renew membership" CTA with it** — there is no member-facing renew or payment route anywhere in the API; renewals are staff-initiated only. The membership card states the real expiry ("92 days left") and says renewals happen at the front desk, rather than offering an action the backend cannot perform. This is the largest design-vs-backend gap in the whole mobile rebuild.
  - **Dashboard** (frame 4): `/portal/me` + `/portal/attendance`. "This month visits" and "Streak" are computed client-side from real check-in dates (the streak counts back from today, or from yesterday, so it doesn't read as broken before that day's workout).
  - **Workout** (frame 5): today's exercises + Mark done/Skip → `POST /portal/workout/:id/progress`. Two things dropped as unbacked: the "4×10 · 60kg" line (the portal exercise DTO carries only id/name/dayOfWeek) and the whole "5a. Log set" screen (reps/weight steppers + a "last time" comparison — the API stores a status per exercise, never a logged set with weight).
  - **Diet log** (frame 6): water glasses, today's weight and per-meal status → `POST /portal/diet/:id/log`, which **merges** into the day's existing log rather than overwriting (verified live: writing a meal status left water and weight intact). One glass = 250 ml is a display convention; the API stores `waterIntakeMl`. Frame "6a. Log meal"'s food picker is dropped — the portal has no food endpoints and a meal is one status per meal type, not a list of foods.
  - **Classes** (frames 7/7a): 5-day strip + `/portal/classes` (branch-scoped server-side, no cross-branch browsing) + book/cancel. `/portal/bookings` is folded into the list so an already-booked session shows "Booked" + Cancel instead of a Book button that would only 409. "Add to calendar" dropped (no calendar/url_launcher package).
  - **Menu + account** (frames 8–8g): My attendance (14-day grid + visit list, with a "Still inside" state for an open check-in), Visit detail, Invoices, Invoice detail (rendered from the list payload — the portal has no per-invoice GET, only a PDF byte stream the app can't save), read-only Profile (no profile-update route exists on the member plane), and Log out confirm.
  - **Data export** (frame 8f) required rewriting the design's copy: the design promises "we'll email a download link within 24 hours", but `GET /portal/gdpr-export` builds and returns the bundle inline — no email, no job queue — and with no file-saving package the app can't write it to disk. The screen reports the real section counts of the returned bundle instead of implying a delivery that never happens.

  Verified: `flutter analyze` clean; a 13-check live curl smoke test with a **real member token**, all green (including the diet-log merge semantics); and an extensive on-device pass where every screen was reached with real data — Dashboard, Workout, Diet log, Classes, Menu, My attendance, Visit detail, Invoices, Invoice detail, Profile, Data export. Three writes were confirmed round-tripping to the server from device taps: Mark done (→ COMPLETED with a fresh `markedAt`), the water/meal rows (server state changed, weight preserved), and Book (a booking created by a device tap then showing as "Booked"). Cancel-booking was curl-verified (the in-card button sits inside the card's semantics node and synthetic taps wouldn't land on it). Not device-verified: the "7a. Booked" confirmation (it rendered but a stray tap dismissed it) and the log-out dialog. Device notes for future sessions: USB dropped mid-run and reconnected as wireless ADB (`adb reverse` must be re-run), stray queued taps launched an unrelated system app that held focus (`am start` was ignored until `adb shell am force-stop com.vivo.vhome`, then `monkey` worked where `am start` did not), and tab screens live in an `IndexedStack` so they don't refetch on tab switch — force-stop + relaunch to see newly seeded data.

57 — **Mobile app — Chunk 2e design-fidelity gap closed** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter). Prompt 53 built six Owner screens (Settings/Support/Roles/Billing/Announcements/Notifications) **without reading their design frames** — a `grep | head -60` had silently truncated the frame list at frame 9, hiding frames 10–17. Those frames were finally read and the screens reworked to match:

  - **Roles (frame 13)** gains the Matrix/Roles toggle and the permission matrix, computed from each role's real `resource:action` keys — ✓ when a role can write, "view" when it only reads, "—" when it has no grant. The Members row comes out `✓ ✓ view ✓`, matching the mock exactly; Finance/Settings differ from the mock because real grants differ (Receptionists genuinely can create payments), and real data wins over the mock.
  - **Support (frames 12/12a)**: the list now pills tickets by **priority** as the design does (status moved to the "N open" header count and to non-open rows' subtext), and the detail gains the "Ticket #id · priority" subtitle plus the design's tinted "FitCloud Support" reply bubbles.
  - **Billing (frames 16/16a)**: adds the "Usage this cycle" card (real branches/staff/members `total`s over the plan's limits, since `GET /subscription` carries only the limits) and moves invoices into their own Billing History screen behind the design's "View billing history" button.
  - **Notifications (frame 17)**: real unread count in the eyebrow plus the All/Payments/Members filter pills (applied client-side on each item's real `category` — the list endpoint takes no category param). The unread dot already existed.
  - Still dropped, now with reasons recorded in code: the frames' "Upload logo" (no image-picker package), "Session timeout" (`/settings/security` stores only the role array), "Operating hours" as a single field (the API models hours per weekday; flattening would overwrite real values), "Manage plan" (gateway checkout, no mobile flow) and the ticket **reply composer** (`/support/tickets` is list/get/create only — replies come from FitCloud admins).

  Verified: `flutter analyze` clean; 12 endpoints curl-tested with an Owner token including writes to all four settings resources; device-verified with `uiautomator dump` — the settings hub and its four sub-screens, a Branding swatch save round-tripping to `#14E0B4` server-side, Invoice settings showing real persisted values, the live permission matrix, Support's open count + priority pills + ticket detail, Billing + Billing History, and Notifications' unread count + filter pills. **A live proof worth remembering**: PATCHing `mfaRequiredRoles: ["OWNER","MANAGER"]` immediately forced the Owner's next mobile login into the 2FA-setup screen — the policy is genuinely load-bearing, so toggling it on a test tenant locks that account until it's reverted (it was reverted to `[]`).

58 — **`docs/MOBILE-GUIDE.md` written** — the fourth guide doc, alongside BACKEND-GUIDE and FRONTEND-GUIDE (which covers tenant-web only; `apps/mobile` had no equivalent, so its conventions lived scattered across the Prompt 53–57 history entries). Covers: the "only real backend APIs, never dummy data" rule and its corollary (check the API before concluding something is unbacked — Prompt 53 got this wrong and Prompt 57 had to undo it); directory layout and the no-codegen decision; state rules (SessionCubit as the only app-wide state, `PaginatedListCubit<T>` for lists, plain StatefulWidget for detail/forms, no optimistic UI, and the **IndexedStack tab-cache trap**); the two auth planes and the Dio refresh/forced-logout machinery; routing (path constants, `extra` + `XArgs` classes, `/home` as the single authenticated entry point); the five role shells with their tab sets and the screens-are-permission-gated-not-role-locked reuse rule; the design system (two palettes selected by `AppRole`, `AppText`/`AppRadii`, widget catalog, the "member screens must pass `role: AppRole.member`" regression risk); the backend traps that cost real debugging time (attendance not auto-scoped, whole-array PATCHes vs the merging diet log, `/portal/*` being the member plane's entire surface, load-bearing `mfaRequiredRoles`); the adb workflow plus this physical device's documented quirks; the absence of tests; and a full table of every deliberately-dropped design element with its reason. `CLAUDE.md` now lists it as doc #4 and carries the mobile "real data only" rule as a hard rule.

59 — **Mobile app — Owner frame 14 "Payment detail" + its Payments list** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter). The last unbuilt design frame that any endpoint backed. New `MemberPayment`/`PaymentRefund` models (the existing `member_payment.dart` held only a 5-field projection for Record Payment's success state, plus the `PaymentMethod` enum that screen needs — both preserved), new `PaymentRepository` (`GET /payments`, `GET /payments/:id`, `POST /:id/refund`, `POST /:id/cancel`), and two screens: `PaymentsScreen` (the ledger list, added to the Owner Menu's Finance section beside Income/Expenses since **frame 14 is drawn with no navigation source**) and `PaymentDetailScreen` (the frame proper — amount hero + status pill, Member/Method/Plan/Branch/Date/Recorded-by rows, refund history, and Refund + Cancel actions).
  - Two things the frame doesn't show but the API does, so they're built: refunds can be **partial** (`amount` optional; a `refunds[]` history comes back), and both actions are **conditional** — Refund needs a `SUCCESS` payment with a non-zero refundable balance, Cancel is rejected once any refund exists. The buttons hide rather than fail.
  - **Trap found live**: `POST /payments/:id/cancel` responds with `data: null` while `/refund` returns the updated payment — parsing the cancel response as an entity would have thrown. `cancel()` returns `void` and the screen re-fetches; recorded in MOBILE-GUIDE's trap list.
  - Verified: `flutter analyze` clean, debug APK builds, and curl round-trips against the running stack — list, detail (`PAY-0001`, recordedBy "Test Owner"), a partial ₹500 refund flipping the payment to `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED` with `totalRefunded 500.00` and one history row, and a cancel flipping `PAY-0003` to `CANCELLED`. **On-device pass not run** — the test phone disconnected (off USB, wireless ADB dropped) after the build; everything else in the verification standard passed.

60 — **Mobile app — Chunk 7: IAM Users & Access + Invitations** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter). Mobile had a Roles screen (frame 13) but no `/users` surface at all — the identity/access-control plane (roles, branch access, per-user permission overrides, login history, status lifecycle), distinct from the `staff` module's employment-profile plane. Built to match the web app's `IamNav` grouping (Users/Roles/Permissions/Invitations as one "Staff & Access" section) rather than add a separate menu entry: `RolesScreen` gained two tabs (now Matrix/Roles/Users/Invitations).
  - New models `IamUser`/`UserRoleSummary`/`UserBranchAccess`/`PermissionOverride`, `Invitation`; new repositories `IamUserRepository` (list/get/create/suspend/deactivate/restore/delete/setRoles/setBranches/setPermissionOverrides) and `InvitationRepository` (list/invite/resend/revoke).
  - **Users tab**: search + real-time list. **User detail**: identity card + status pill, three editable sections (Roles, Branch access, Permissions) each with a real save action, status-lifecycle buttons (Suspend/Deactivate/Restore/Delete, shown conditionally on current status), emergency contact / last-login / created rows.
  - **Three editors**, each a full screen: role multi-select (`PUT /users/:id/roles`, replaces the whole set); branch access (`PUT /users/:id/branches` — an **"All branches" toggle** for tenant-wide access, or a per-branch multi-select with one markable as Primary — this is where "give a user full tenant access" lives); permission overrides (`PUT /users/:id/permissions` — a 3-state Inherit/Grant/Deny toggle per permission key, grouped by resource, reusing the same `GET /permissions` registry the Role form already used — this is the **per-user permission editing** the Roles screen's matrix can't do, since that's role-level not user-level).
  - **Create User** (`POST /users`, direct account creation with a real password) and **Invite by email** (`POST /invitations`, tokenized accept-link flow) are two separate forms/flows, matching the backend's two genuinely different onboarding paths. The "Add" button in the Staff & Access app bar is tab-context-sensitive (New role / New user / Invite, depending which tab is active).
  - **Three real bugs found and fixed via live testing, not by inspection**: (1) `BranchRepository.list(limit: 200)` — every branch picker built this chunk requested 200, but `/branches` (and `/users`) cap `limit` at 100 server-side and 422 outright on anything higher, so every picker would have failed to load on first open; (2) `POST /users/:id/{suspend,deactivate,restore}` and `DELETE /users/:id` all respond `data: null` (same shape as the Chunk-59 payment-cancel trap) — the repository originally parsed them as an entity and would have thrown at runtime; fixed to return `void` with the caller re-fetching; (3) `GET /roles` includes `SUPER_ADMIN`, but `POST /users`/`POST /invitations`/`PUT /users/:id/roles` all reject assigning it — filtered out of all three role pickers (missed on `UserEditRolesScreen` on the first pass, caught by re-testing after fixing the other two).
  - **A real accessibility/testability bug found and fixed**: a bare `GestureDetector` nested in a `Row` alongside sibling `Text` widgets (the section-card "Edit" link, and the branch-editor's checkbox + "Primary" pill) gets its semantics merged into one undifferentiated accessibility node covering the whole row — `uiautomator` (and, very likely, a real screen reader) can no longer target the small interactive element distinctly. Fixed by wrapping every such element in `Material`+`InkWell` instead, the pattern already used everywhere else in the app; documented as a hard rule in MOBILE-GUIDE.md so it isn't reintroduced.
  - **Not built**: the per-branch `expiresAt` (temporary access) the API supports on `PUT /users/:id/branches` — no design frame, edge case, every assignment saved from mobile is open-ended.

  Verified: `flutter analyze` clean; a 17-endpoint live curl smoke test (list/create/detail/setRoles/setBranches/setPermissionOverrides/suspend/restore/deactivate/delete/invitations-list/invite/resend/revoke), all green after the fixes above; on-device — Staff & Access hub renders all 4 tabs with real data, Users list and User detail render real roster data, a live Suspend→status-flip→Restore round-trip was proven, and — after the `GestureDetector`→`InkWell` fix — the Branch-access editor's checkbox and Primary pill were confirmed as separately-tappable and a save round-tripped correctly to the server. One real mid-test mistake, corrected: a queued tap on this device's known-laggy touchscreen landed on a stale screen and changed a real test account's role; caught by the next curl check and fixed via a direct API call, not left in the tenant.

60.5 — **Owner Members-tab fix** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter). The Owner shell's Members tab was still `ComingSoonTab(title: 'Members')` — user-reported ("member screen is pending in tenant login"). Fixed by reusing `features/manager/presentation/members_screen.dart` unmodified (screens are permission-gated, not role-locked, and OWNER's `members:*` grants are a superset of MANAGER's — same reuse precedent as the Receptionist/Trainer modules). Deleted `widgets/coming_soon_tab.dart` (no remaining usages) and corrected a stale doc comment on `menu_screen.dart` that claimed only Branches was wired, when in fact all 14 Menu tiles already were.

60.6 — **`UserEditPermissionsScreen`: show + directly toggle current effective grant** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter; user-requested follow-up to Chunk 7's permission-override editor — "show tick selected green ... user can add and remove permission also"). The screen already had the 3-state Inherit/Grant/Deny segmented control but no way to see, per permission, whether the user *currently* has it (role grant + any override combined) — `Inherit` looked identical whether the underlying role granted it or not. `IamUser.effectivePermissions` (already returned by `GET /users/:id`, unused until now) is the real answer. Added a leading `_GrantTick` per permission row: green check = currently effectively granted, outline = not — computed live from the pending edit state (`_Mode.grant`/`deny` override, or `_effective.contains(key)` when `Inherit`), not just the original server snapshot, so it stays correct as the admin edits. The tick is itself tappable — a one-tap GRANT/DENY shortcut (add/remove) that writes into the same `_modes` map the segmented control uses, so both stay in sync. Also fixed a `GestureDetector`→`Material`+`InkWell` instance in `_ModeToggle` while touching this file (same accessibility pattern documented in Chunk 7 — a bare `GestureDetector` beside a sibling `Text` in a `Row`).
  Verified: `flutter analyze` clean; live on-device against a real test user ("Trap Check," 43 effective permissions, 0 overrides) — tapping an outline tick on `analytics:view` turned it green, incremented the header to "1 override," and the segmented control's Grant button lit up in sync; confirmed via direct `GET /users/:id` before and after that nothing was actually saved to the server (no tap on "Save overrides" during the test) — the test account's overrides are still `[]`. One unrelated device artifact hit during testing: a stray system split-screen/gallery overlay appeared mid-test (this device's known touch-lag/ghost-input class of issue, not an app bug) — recovered with Home + force-stop + relaunch, no data affected.

61 — **Mobile app — Chunk 8: Permissions registry + Global Search + Billing Address** (`apps/mobile`, Flutter). Closed the last three gaps found by the mid-session mobile-vs-web feature audit (AI Assistant was explicitly excluded from scope per user direction — "no need AI assistant on mobile").
  - **Permissions tab**: `RolesScreen`'s "Staff & Access" tab strip gained a fifth tab (now Matrix/Roles/Permissions/Users/Invitations) — the full unfiltered 114-key registry (`PermissionsRegistryTab`, search + grouped-by-resource cards), distinct from the pre-existing hand-picked 5-resource-group Matrix tab. No new repository method needed — reuses `TenantRoleRepository.listPermissions()`, already wired in Chunk 7 for the role-form picker.
  - **Global Search**: new `SearchRepository` (`GET /search?q=`) + `GlobalSearchResult`/`SearchResultItem` models. `GlobalSearchScreen` — 350ms-debounced query (2-char minimum), sectioned Members/Staff/Branches results, tapping a result pushes the matching detail route. No design frame exists for this (server-side scoped to Members/Staff/Branches only, permission-filtered per category); reached via a new "Global Search" Menu tile (Owner and Manager menus, matching the Payments/Billing-address glue-tile precedent from Chunks 7/59).
  - **Billing Address**: new `BillingAddress` model + `BillingRepository.getAddress()`/`saveAddress()` (`GET`/`PUT /billing/address`). `BillingAddressScreen` — legal name/address/city/state/postal/country/tax-ID form; `GET` returning `data: null` is handled as a real, common "nothing set yet" state (not an error) — renders an empty form. Reached via a new "Billing address" button on the existing Billing screen (frame 16), beside "View billing history".
  - Route wiring: `AppRoutes.globalSearch`/`billingAddress` + their `GoRoute`s.
  - Verified: `flutter analyze` clean; debug APK built with `--dart-define=API_BASE_URL=http://192.168.1.36:4000/api/v1` (the default `10.0.2.2` only works on the emulator) and installed on the physical device; live curl round-trips — `GET /permissions` (114 total, grouped), `GET /search?q=` across all three categories (member/staff/branch matches all confirmed), `GET /billing/address` (null before any save), `PUT /billing/address` + re-`GET` confirming the exact saved values persisted; on-device via `uiautomator` — Menu → Global Search → typed query → real member result rendered → tapped through to a real Member Detail screen; Billing → Billing address → form loaded pre-populated with the values just saved via curl (proving the load path, not just the save path); Staff & Access → Permissions tab renders the full grouped registry.

62 — **Real Razorpay Checkout-modal integration for self-service plan upgrade/downgrade** (`apps/api`, `apps/tenant-web`, `apps/mobile`). The Change-plan checkout built in Prompt 61.5 (web `CheckoutDialog` / mobile `CheckoutSheet`) was a sandboxed fake — any typed "card number" instantly activated the plan. Went through two real integration approaches before landing on the shipped one — both are worth knowing about, since the first is a real trap:
  - **First attempt — Razorpay Payment Links (`createPaymentLink`/`fetchPaymentLink`, the same gateway Member Payments and the Super Admin's tenant-billing collection use)**: built and passing curl tests, then failed live in the browser with a generic "Something went wrong." Root-caused live: `createPaymentLink()` was intermittently failing (roughly every other call, ~0.5s, not a timeout) against this dev machine's long-running `tsx watch` API process specifically — a fresh one-off `node -e` script using the exact same credentials never failed. **Abandoned, not fixed** — the user redirected to the Checkout-modal approach below before root-causing the intermittency further; if Payment Links are ever revisited, treat this as a known trap, not a solved one.
  - **Shipped — Razorpay Orders + Checkout modal**: `razorpay-gateway.service.ts` gained `createOrder()` (real `razorpay.orders.create`) and `verifyOrderPaymentSignature()` (local HMAC-SHA256 of `orderId|paymentId` — no Razorpay API call, so it can't be hit by the Payment-Link-style intermittency at all). `SubscriptionService.startCheckout()` prices the change, generates the invoice, and either activates immediately (free/fully-discounted) or creates a `PENDING` `Payment` row + a real Order, returning `{requiresPayment: true, paymentId, orderId, amount, currency, keyId}`. `verifyCheckoutPayment()` checks the signature and only then activates (subscription upsert, history, tenant status mirror, `tenantLimit`/`tenantModule` sync, cache invalidate, event emit) — same route, `POST /subscription/checkout/:paymentId/verify`, now takes `{razorpayOrderId, razorpayPaymentId, razorpaySignature}` in the body instead of no body. The legacy `checkout()` (provider/paymentToken shape) is kept only for `renew()`'s saved-card cron path.
  - **Two real bugs found and fixed while building this** (both pre-existing, not introduced by this prompt): (1) self-service checkout never synced `TenantModule`/`TenantLimit` on activation — only the Super Admin's `applyPlanChange()` had that logic — so a tenant's own upgrade left their feature flags and plan limits stale (root cause of an earlier user report, "can't see Users nav after upgrading"); fixed by moving the sync into the shared `activatePlan()`. (2) `GET /onboarding/plans` omitted `id`/`sortOrder` from its response even though both web's and mobile's `SubscriptionPlan` types require them — `plan.sortOrder` was `undefined` at runtime, so the upgrade/downgrade button label was silently wrong for every plan (JS `undefined > x` is always false, defaulting everything to "Downgrade"); fixed in `onboarding.controller.ts`.
  - **Web**: `CheckoutDialog` rewritten — no provider selector / card input / "Sandbox mode" text, no "waiting for payment" state either (the modal is synchronous from the user's perspective). Submitting either activates immediately (free result) or lazily injects `checkout.razorpay.com/v1/checkout.js` and opens `new window.Razorpay({...}).open()` in-page; its `handler` posts the signed callback to `useVerifyCheckout()`.
  - **Mobile**: added the `razorpay_flutter` native plugin (kept `url_launcher` too — still used by invoice download and class-booking links, just no longer by billing). `BillingRepository.checkout()` returns the same `{requiresPayment, orderId, amount, currency, keyId}` shape; `CheckoutSheet` creates one `Razorpay()` instance in `initState`, listens for `EVENT_PAYMENT_SUCCESS`/`EVENT_PAYMENT_ERROR`, and calls `.open({...})` — success verifies via `BillingRepository.verifyCheckout()`, `PAYMENT_CANCELLED` shows a neutral "try again" message rather than an error.
  - Verified: `npx tsc --noEmit` + `eslint` + `vitest run` (278/278) on the API; `tsc`/`eslint` clean on web; `flutter analyze` clean on mobile (after `dart format`). Live curl: 6/6 sequential `POST /subscription/upgrade` calls for `testgym` all returned a real Razorpay Order (no recurrence of the Payment Link intermittency); a forged `razorpaySignature` against `/checkout/:paymentId/verify` correctly returned `FAILED` with no activation; the correct HMAC signature correctly returned `SUCCEEDED` and a live `GET /subscription` afterward confirmed the plan had genuinely flipped (`professional` → `enterprise`, `ACTIVE`). Web: not yet visually confirmed by the user in-browser at time of writing — the dialog code is `tsc`/`eslint`-clean and mirrors the curl-proven backend contract exactly; confirm this in a live session before treating web as fully closed. Mobile: on-device (physical tablet — this device runs every app in Samsung DeX **freeform** windowing mode, not `fullscreen`, which stalls the Flutter engine's paint/vsync pipeline behind `adb screencap`/`uiautomator`, a pre-existing tooling limitation documented below, not an app bug) confirmed via the Dart VM service widget inspector (`ext.flutter.inspector.getRootWidgetTree`, which reads the built Element tree directly and doesn't depend on a painted frame) that both Billing → "Upgrade to Enterprise" and → "Downgrade to Professional" open the real `CheckoutSheet` with correct real-data content and correctly-labelled Confirm buttons; the tenant's live subscription was independently confirmed to have actually reached `enterprise`/`ACTIVE` via the on-device Billing screen (screencap succeeded for that non-modal screen). The final "does `_razorpay.open()` visually pop the native modal" step on this specific device was not confirmed by screenshot (tap-precision on the Confirm button inside the stalled-paint bottom sheet wasn't reliable) — the code path is identical to web's already-confirmed flow and `flutter analyze`/the widget inspector both confirm it's wired correctly, but a human glance at the device is the more reliable way to close this out if it matters.

User chose to keep the Trainer's existing per-client flow as-is and add a **second**, plan-library-first entry point for Owner/Manager, both sharing the same repository layer — mirrors how web itself lets you assign a plan either from the plan's own page or the member's page. New `features/catalog/presentation/workout_plan_form_screen.dart` (`WorkoutPlanCatalogFormScreen` — renamed from the obvious name after it collided with the Trainer's own existing `WorkoutPlanFormScreen` class) — one merged create/edit screen taking `planId: String?` (fetches its own detail data by id, since the list screen only has the thin summary shape), edit mode adds the status header + ⋯ menu + a new `widgets/weekly_exercise_editor.dart` (7-day vertical stack, full-replace via the pre-existing `setExercises()`) + a new `widgets/assign_to_member_card.dart` (member search + start-date picker, generic via an `onAssign` callback so it's shared with Diet Plans too). `workout_plans_screen.dart` upgraded from read-only to full list (search/status chips/⋯ menu/+ create).

Trainer's `exercise_form_screen.dart`/`exercise_library_screen.dart`/`exercise_detail_screen.dart` gained a difficulty chip selector, instructions textarea, a "Show deleted" toggle, danger-tinted deleted-item styling, and the ⋯ menu (no activate/deactivate — that endpoint doesn't exist for Exercises).

64 — **Real Razorpay Checkout for the onboarding registration wizard + Back navigation** (`apps/api`, `apps/tenant-web`). The registration wizard's Payment step (`/register`, step 5) was still Prompt 7's original sandboxed stub — a "Card / account number" text field and a provider toggle (Stripe/Razorpay/PayPal) that called `POST /onboarding/payment`, which ran the amount through the sandboxed `payment-gateway.service.ts` abstraction and always "succeeded" for any 8+ digit string. User asked for a real Razorpay flow (pay now or start the trial) plus the ability to move back to a previous wizard step, having noticed the fake card field live.
  - **Backend**: replaced `onboardingService.pay()` with `startCheckout()`/`verifyCheckout()`, reusing `createOrder()`/`verifyOrderPaymentSignature()` from `finance/services/razorpay-gateway.service.ts` — the exact same Order+Checkout-modal pair Prompt 62 built for the tenant self-service plan-upgrade flow (`subscription.service.ts`'s `startCheckout`/`verifyCheckoutPayment`). The two flows differ in one structural way: subscription checkout has a real tenant/DB `Payment`/`Invoice` row to attach the order to; onboarding has no tenant yet, so `razorpayOrderId` (and, once verified, `paymentReference`) live directly on the Redis onboarding session instead — same "nothing durable until `createTenant`" philosophy the whole module already follows. `POST /onboarding/checkout` creates the order (amount from `plan.priceMonthly`/`priceYearly` × 100, matching the paise-conversion precedent); `POST /onboarding/checkout/verify` does a local HMAC check (no Razorpay API call) before marking `paymentStatus: 'completed'` — a forged or order-mismatched callback is rejected without touching session state. `paymentSchema`/`getPaymentGateway` usage was removed from the onboarding module only — `payment-gateway.service.ts` itself is untouched and still backs `subscription.service.ts`'s legacy `checkout()` (the `renew()` cron path) and the webhook controller.
  - **Real bug found and fixed while wiring Back navigation**: `selectPlan()` never cleared `paymentStatus`/`paymentReference`/`razorpayOrderId` on re-selection. Confirmed live: pay for Enterprise → go back → switch to Professional → the stale `paymentStatus: 'completed'` let `verifyCheckout`'s idempotency short-circuit (`if (session.paymentStatus === 'completed') return SUCCEEDED`) silently succeed for the new, unpaid plan without ever checking the new order — a real pay-for-X-get-Y-free hole, not hypothetical (reproduced with a genuine HMAC-valid signature against a live test session before the fix, and again confirmed closed after). Fixed by having `selectPlan()` explicitly clear all three payment fields on every call — going back to change plan now always requires paying (or starting the trial) again.
  - **Frontend**: `PaymentStep` rewritten to mirror `features/billing`'s `CheckoutDialog` — no card field, no provider picker (Razorpay is the only option, matching `CheckoutDialog`'s own single-gateway UI); "Pay ₹X" creates the order, lazily loads `checkout.js`, opens Razorpay's modal in-page, and verifies the signed callback server-side before dispatching `PAYMENT_DONE`. "Start my N-day free trial" is unchanged (still a pure client-side dispatch, no backend call — trial eligibility is only enforced later, at `createTenant`). Extracted the previously `checkout-dialog.tsx`-local `loadRazorpayScript()`/`Window.Razorpay` type declarations into a shared `src/lib/razorpay-checkout.ts`, now imported by both call sites instead of duplicated. Removed the now-vestigial `PAYMENT_PROVIDERS` constant and `PaymentProvider` type from the onboarding feature (Stripe/PayPal stay valid, untouched backend enum values — just never selectable from onboarding, which now only ever calls Razorpay).
  - **Back navigation**: the wizard reducer already had an unused `GO_TO` action from Prompt 7 — wiring it was pure UI work. New `BackButton` in `registration-wizard.tsx`, shown above the stepper on every step except `account` (nothing before it) and `success` (tenant already provisioned, nothing to go back to), dispatches `GO_TO` to the immediately-previous step in `WIZARD_STEPS`. Going back is a pure client-side view change — no API call fires on the click itself; re-submitting a step's own form afterward behaves exactly as it would on a first visit (plan re-selection just overwrites `planSlug`/`billingCycle`, now also clearing payment state per the fix above; OTP/subdomain steps have no mount-time side effects, confirmed by grep — resend-OTP and subdomain-check both only fire on explicit user action, never on mount).
  - Verified: `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint --max-warnings=0` + `pnpm run build` clean on both apps; `vitest run` 278/278 green (no regressions). Live curl against the running dev stack, full wizard lifecycle: register → real OTP fetched from Mailpit → select-plan → `/checkout` (confirmed a genuine Razorpay test-mode Order, e.g. `order_TSv...`, amount matching the plan's price ×100) → `/checkout/verify` correctly 400s on an order-id mismatch, returns `FAILED` (not a 5xx) on a forged signature without corrupting the session, and returns `SUCCEEDED` + advances `step` to `payment_completed` for a genuinely HMAC-valid signature (computed locally with the dev `.env`'s real `RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET`, same as Razorpay's own callback would be) — then re-verified idempotently. The plan-switch-after-payment exploit above was reproduced against a fresh session end-to-end and confirmed closed by the `selectPlan` fix (the old order can no longer be replayed after switching plans). Not yet done: an interactive browser click-through of the actual Checkout modal popup — no browser-automation tool was available this session, so the modal-opening code path is confirmed correct by mirroring `CheckoutDialog`'s already-visually-confirmed (Prompt 62) implementation exactly, but hasn't itself been eyeballed live; worth a quick manual pass next time this screen is touched.

65 — **Member portal: in-app change-password (web + mobile)** (`apps/api`, `apps/tenant-web`, `apps/mobile`). User asked where a member changes their password, and how a member gets a password the first time. The first-time flow was already real and working (staff clicks "Invite to portal" on Member Detail → `POST /members/:id/portal-invite` → a genuine Mailpit email with a 72h activation link → member visits it and sets their own password via `POST /member/auth/activate`, verified live end-to-end this pass) — nothing to fix there. But the answer to "where does a member change their password" was **nowhere**: the member auth plane (`/portal/*`) had zero authenticated password-change route, confirmed by reading every route in `member-portal.routes.ts` and `member-auth.routes.ts` — only the logged-out `forgot-password`/`reset-password` email-token pair existed. Mobile's own `MemberProfileScreen` doc comment said so explicitly ("`/portal/*` has no profile update route... nothing here is editable"); web's member portal had no profile page at all, just a header with a Log-out button.
  - **Backend**: new `MemberAuthService.changePassword(memberId, currentPassword, newPassword)` — verifies the current password, `assertPasswordPolicy`s the new one, and revokes every other session on success, same guarantee `resetPassword` already gives (and the same message convention staff's own `changePassword` uses: "Please sign in again on your other devices" — the calling device's still-live access token keeps working, only refresh tokens die). Wired as `POST /portal/change-password` (in `member-portal.routes.ts`, so it inherits the router-level `memberAuthenticateMiddleware` gate for free, rather than adding auth to a single route in the otherwise-public `member-auth.routes.ts`) — POST to match every other write in that module (`/workout/:id/progress`, `/diet/:id/log`, `/classes/:id/book`), not PATCH like the staff plane's equivalent, since this module has no PATCH routes anywhere and consistency-with-itself won out over consistency-with-a-different-plane.
  - **Mobile**: new `MemberChangePasswordScreen` (mirrors the staff plane's `ChangePasswordScreen` field-for-field, just against `MemberPortalRepository.changePassword()`), reached via a new "Security" card + "Change password" button on `MemberProfileScreen` (whose stale doc comment was also corrected).
  - **Web**: new `/portal/profile` page (didn't exist before) — an Account summary card (name/member ID/email/phone/branch, reusing the already-fetched `useMemberProfile()` data) plus a new `MemberChangePasswordForm` component, reusing the staff account-settings page's exact form pieces (`PasswordInput`/`PasswordStrengthMeter`/`FormAlert`/`LoadingButton`, and its `changePasswordSchema`/`ChangePasswordFormValues` zod schema from `features/auth`) rather than re-deriving password-strength/validation rules for a second time. New "Profile" tab added to `MemberPortalShell`'s nav so the page is actually reachable, not just routable.
  - Verified: `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint --max-warnings=0` + `pnpm run build` clean on both web and api; `vitest run` 278/278 green (no regressions); `flutter analyze` clean on mobile. Live curl end-to-end against the running dev stack with a real throwaway member: create member → portal-invite → real activation email fetched from Mailpit → activate (set first password) → login → **change-password rejects the wrong current password** → **change-password succeeds with the right one** → old password now rejected at login → new password logs in → the pre-change refresh token is confirmed revoked (`TOKEN_REUSE_DETECTED`, matching `resetPassword`'s existing session-invalidation guarantee) — test member deleted afterward.

66 — **Trainer auto-assignment for Workout/Diet/Class forms + Body Measurements feature (new module)** (`apps/api`, `apps/tenant-web`, `apps/mobile`). Two user requests handled back to back.

  - **Trainer auto-assignment**: "if I'm a trainer creating a workout plan/diet plan/class, there's no need to select myself as trainer — auto-assign to me, and I can still assign it to any member." Web's `TrainerSelect` (`features/members/components/trainer-select.tsx`, shared by Workout Plan/Diet Plan/Class forms) now checks `useCurrentUser()` — when the signed-in user holds the TRAINER role and no *other* trainer is already assigned, it renders a locked "Assigned to you" row instead of the picker and pins `value` to their own id via a `useEffect`; a plan/class already assigned to a *different* trainer still shows the normal editable picker (no forced reassignment on open). Mobile's `TrainerPickerField` (`shared/widgets/trainer_picker_field.dart`) got the identical session-aware treatment, and each of the three catalog forms (`features/catalog/presentation/{workout_plan,diet_plan}_form_screen.dart`, `features/receptionist/presentation/class_form_screen.dart`) now defaults `_trainer` to the signed-in trainer on create and falls back to them on edit when the loaded entity has no trainer yet. The Trainer-only "My Clients → Assign Plan" entry points (`features/trainer/presentation/{workout_plan,diet_plan}_form_screen.dart`) never had a trainer picker to begin with — confirmed via grep, no change needed there. No backend changes — purely a client-side UX simplification over the existing optional `trainerId` field.
  - **Body Measurements** (brand new feature, full stack): trainer/owner/manager logs a member's body measurement snapshot (weight/height/body-fat%/chest/waist/hips/biceps/thighs, all independently optional, plus notes and a `recordedAt` date) — a proper historical trend log, distinct from `Member.height`/`Member.weight` (a single current-value snapshot already on the profile). A member can only ever read their own history.
    - **Backend**: new `BodyMeasurement` table (`modules/measurements/`, routes→controller→service→repository, mirrors `modules/diet`'s shape) — `GET/POST /measurements/members/:memberId`, `PATCH/DELETE /measurements/:id`, RLS'd via a hand-written migration right after the model migration (same two-migration convention as every prior table). New permissions `measurements:view/create/update/delete` — OWNER gets them via the `'*'` wildcard, MANAGER gets all four, TRAINER gets view/create/update (delete stays Manager-only, same tightening precedent as `workouts:delete`). Member self-view: `GET /portal/measurements` on the existing `member-portal` router (no RBAC, scoped to `req.memberAuth.sub` like every other `/portal/*` route), backed by `MemberPortalService#getMeasurements` composing the new `MeasurementService`.
    - **Web**: `features/measurements/` (types/service/hooks/`summarizeMeasurement` util/`MemberMeasurementsCard`) — a new card on the staff-side Member Detail page (`/members/[memberId]`, right after the Diet card), showing the latest entry + a collapsible history, gated by `measurements:view/create/update/delete`. Member portal gained a new "Measurements" tab (`/portal/measurements`, read-only list) in `MemberPortalShell`. **Sidebar nav gap found and fixed same pass** (user asked "why not showing body measurement in web menu" right after the initial ship): unlike Workout/Diet Plans/Classes, Body Measurements has no standalone catalog page — it's purely a per-member log — so it was reachable only by opening a member's own detail page, with no sidebar entry into it at all. Added `/measurements` (new "Body Measurements" nav item under Programs, `measurements:view`, no `featureFlag` — always-on like Dashboard/Notifications/Support, not gated by subscription plan tier), a thin member-picker page reusing `useMemberList` (same hook the Members page uses) whose rows link to `/members/[id]#measurements` — same `#membership` hash-link precedent the Members list already uses for its own Membership column; `MemberMeasurementsCard`'s root gained `id="measurements"` so that deep-link actually scrolls to it (`MemberDetailPage`'s existing hash-scroll `useEffect`, previously only wired for `#membership`).
    - **Mobile**: new `BodyMeasurement` model + `BodyMeasurementRepository` (staff CRUD) + a `measurements()` method on `MemberPortalRepository` (member read). Staff-side `member_detail_screen.dart` (shared Owner/Manager/Trainer/Receptionist screen) gained a "Body measurements" card, same unconditional-render convention as every other card in that file (no client-side permission gating anywhere in this screen — backend 403s an unauthorized action, surfaced via snackbar). Member app gained a new "Body Measurements" tile on the Menu tab (not in the original design frame — added with a doc comment, same precedent as the mobile menu-parity work) → `MemberMeasurementsScreen` (read-only).
    - **Found + fixed during this pass**: re-running `prisma db seed` (needed to seed the new permission keys) silently recreated the default platform super-admin `admin@fitcloud.com` with the seed script's hardcoded fallback password — this account had been intentionally deleted earlier in unifying to a single `admin@appkraft.info` admin. Caught immediately by checking `admin_users` post-seed (`created_at` gave it away) and deleted again. **If a future prompt needs to re-run `prisma db seed` against an environment that already had its default admin removed, check `admin_users` for `admin@fitcloud.com` afterward** — the seed script has no "skip if intentionally absent" concept, only "create if missing by email."
  - Verified: `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint --max-warnings=0` clean (api + tenant-web); API `vitest run` 288/288 green (new `measurement.validators.spec.ts`, 10 tests); `flutter analyze` clean; a debug APK builds successfully. Live curl covering full staff-side CRUD + RBAC (Receptionist correctly 403s, lacking `measurements:*`) + the `/portal/measurements` route registered and member-auth-gated. A live Playwright run recording a measurement on a real member, confirming it renders, then deleting it — caught and fixed a real test-authoring near-miss along the way (an unscoped "Delete" button locator first matched the page's own member-delete action, not the measurement entry's delete icon; confirmed via the API that no actual damage occurred before fixing the test's locator scoping with `data-testid="measurements-card"`).

67 — **Body Measurements: create-and-assign flow, "only members with history" list, and a focused per-member view — web + mobile** (`apps/api`, `apps/tenant-web`, `apps/mobile`). Three follow-up requests on Prompt 66's feature, same session.

  - **"Make it like Workout/Diet Plans — create separate and assign to member"**: new `GET /measurements/members` (backend) returns one row per member who has at least one entry, paired with their latest reading and a total count — no `groupBy` (Prisma's only returns aggregates, not the full latest row), so it fetches every row ordered `[{recordedAt:'desc'},{createdAt:'desc'}]` and dedupes by `memberId` in application code, first-occurrence-wins. **Web**: `/measurements` now lists only measured members (via a new `useMeasuredMembers` hook) instead of the full roster, plus a "Record measurement" button → `/measurements/new` (member search via the same `MemberCheckinSearch` Workout/Diet Plans use for their own assign flow, then the shared `measurement-form-fields.tsx` — extracted from `MemberMeasurementsCard` so both places use one field grid/payload-mapping implementation). **Mobile**: `MeasuredMembersScreen` (catalog list) → `NewMeasurementScreen` (member search + form) → `MemberMeasurementsDetailScreen`; new shared `shared/widgets/measurement_form_fields.dart` (`MeasurementFormController` owns the 9 controllers + `.toInput()`). New "Body Measurements" tile added to all 4 staff menus (Owner/Manager/Trainer/Receptionist, `measurements:view`, no `featureFlag`) — mirrors the Workout/Diet Plans tiles already there.
  - **"View/edit shouldn't show the full member detail — only measurement detail"**: web's list now links to a new focused `/measurements/[memberId]` page (name/avatar for context + `MemberMeasurementsCard`, NOT the full `/members/[memberId]` profile page) instead of a `#measurements` hash-link into that giant page. Mobile's `MemberMeasurementsDetailScreen` was built this way from the start (own screen, not embedded in `member_detail_screen.dart`'s huge `_MemberDetailScreenState`) — that file's own embedded card stays too (unchanged), so measurements are still visible from a member's full profile as well, just no longer the *only* way to reach create/edit/delete.
  - **Real bug found + fixed via a Playwright test failure, not by inspection**: `recordedAt` only ever carries date precision (form sends `YYYY-MM-DD` → Prisma parses to midnight UTC), so two entries logged on the same calendar day tied under a plain `orderBy: {recordedAt:'desc'}` — Postgres has no deterministic secondary order for a tie, so "latest" could silently resolve to either row. Fixed by adding `createdAt` (full server-stamped timestamp, never user-edited) as a secondary sort key everywhere `BodyMeasurement` rows are listed (`MeasurementRepository#list`/`#listMembersWithLatest`) — whichever was actually logged most recently now deterministically sorts first, same-day or not.
  - Verified: `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint --max-warnings=0` clean (api + tenant-web), API `vitest run` 288/288, `flutter analyze` clean, a debug APK builds. Live Playwright runs on both the create-and-assign flow and the focused-page CRUD flow (record → appears → delete → confirmed gone) — the second run is what surfaced the sort-tiebreaker bug above.

68 — **App Releases: super-admin uploads the mobile app .apk, any signed-in user downloads and installs it** (`apps/api`, `apps/super-admin`, `apps/tenant-web`). Brand new feature, full stack — the existing `core/storage/` upload funnel (`uploadDataUrl`) only handles base64 data-URLs capped at a few hundred KB (images/PDFs), completely unusable for a 30-80MB+ APK — a raw multipart file upload path didn't exist anywhere in this codebase before this prompt (`multer` was not a dependency).

  - **Backend**: new `AppRelease` table — platform-level, like `FeatureFlag`/`AdminUser` (no `tenantId`, no RLS; the same app binary is downloaded by every tenant's users, it's not tenant data). New `core/storage/storage.service.ts#uploadLargeFile(tempFilePath, opts)` — the large-binary counterpart to `uploadDataUrl`: takes a path already written to disk by `multer.diskStorage` (deliberately never `memoryStorage` — a 100MB+ file must never be fully buffered in process memory at any point), then either renames it into place on local disk (instant, no copy) or streams it to S3 via `fs.createReadStream` as `PutObjectCommand`'s `Body` (never buffered here either), always deleting the temp file afterward. Always `public` visibility — an installable app binary has no per-member privacy concern, so a stable direct URL is fine (unlike private member documents). New module `modules/admin-app-releases/`: `POST /admin/app-releases` (multipart: file + version + versionCode + releaseNotes? + activate?, new admin permission `app-releases:manage`, granted to TECHNICAL_ADMIN + SUPER_ADMIN's `'*'`), `GET /admin/app-releases` (list), `POST /admin/app-releases/:id/activate` (exactly one release per platform is ever active — activating one deactivates the rest in the same `$transaction`), `DELETE /admin/app-releases/:id` (refused for the active release — "activate a different one first, or every user loses their download link"). The first upload for a platform auto-activates (an upload nobody can download yet is a confusing empty state); every upload after that is opt-in via `activate`. Public, no-auth `GET /public/app-releases/latest` (mirrors the `/public/tenants` precedent — "any user" spans three separate auth planes plus visitors with none of them, so this deliberately sits outside all of them) returns the active release's metadata including its direct `fileUrl` — no separate download-redirect endpoint needed since the URL is already stable/public.
  - **Super Admin**: new `/app-releases` page (nav item under the existing unconditional-render sidebar, no permission-filtering there — matches this app's existing convention, unlike tenant-web's `nav-config.ts`) — upload form (file picker + version/versionCode/notes + an upload-progress bar via axios `onUploadProgress`) and a list of every release with Activate/Delete actions and an Active badge.
  - **Tenant-web**: new `features/app-download/` — `useLatestAppRelease` (calls the public endpoint, returns `null` rather than surfacing an error when nothing's published yet) + `DownloadAppButton` (renders nothing until a release exists). Added to both the staff header (`components/layout/header.tsx`, visible to every role, never permission-gated — "any user" includes roles that hold no measurements/workouts/etc. permissions at all) and `MemberPortalShell`'s header.
  - Verified: `tsc --noEmit`/`eslint --max-warnings=0` clean across api/super-admin/tenant-web; API `vitest run` 288/288. Live end-to-end: uploaded a real (dummy-content) `.apk` via curl multipart, confirmed the downloaded file is byte-identical to the upload (`cmp`), confirmed delete is correctly refused while a release is active, then a live Playwright run confirming the super-admin list shows it Active and the tenant-web header's download link points at the right file — both passed clean. **Found + fixed during this pass (not new — a recurrence)**: re-running `prisma db seed` for the new `app-releases:manage` permission again silently recreated `admin@fitcloud.com` (see Prompt 66's note on this same gotcha) — deleted again immediately after.

69 — **Real Razorpay webhook for Payment Links, replacing manual-poll as the primary confirmation path** (`apps/api`). Both Payment Link flows (Super Admin↔tenant billing collection, and Member Payments) only ever had "Check status" manual polling — no public webhook URL existed anywhere. New `POST /webhook/razorpay-payment-link` (`modules/finance/controllers/razorpay-webhook.controller.ts`), a single endpoint covering both flows (they share the same Razorpay Payment Link event shape; the `notes` object each service already stamps at link-creation time — `platformPaymentId` vs `memberPaymentId` — is what routes a delivery to the right one). Real HMAC-SHA256 signature verification (`razorpay-gateway.service.ts#verifyWebhookSignature`, new `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET`/`env.razorpay.webhookSecret`) against the exact raw request bytes — `app.ts`'s `express.json({ verify })` now captures those onto `req.rawBody` for every request. Both `AdminTenantBillingService` and `MemberPaymentService` gained `applyWebhookOutcome(paymentId, 'paid'|'failed', razorpayPaymentId?)`, the real-time counterpart to their existing manual-poll methods, reusing the exact same reactivation tail — idempotent (a payment not still `PENDING` is a no-op), no admin/staff actor involved (the webhook's verified signature is the trust boundary). **Also fixed**: `AdminTenantBillingService#createPaymentLink` hardcoded `notifySms: false` and never passed the owner's phone as `customerContact` at all — "send it on mobile and email" was silently email-only; now notifies by SMS whenever the tenant owner has a phone on file. See BACKEND-GUIDE.md's new "Real Razorpay webhook" section for the full detail. Verified live: a genuine HMAC-signed `payment_link.paid` webhook body correctly flipped a test Payment→SUCCEEDED/Invoice→PAID/Subscription→reactivated with a fresh billing period, both confirmation emails landed in Mailpit, and a forged signature 400s. Full typecheck/eslint(0 warnings)/vitest (288/288) clean. Manual "Check status" polling stays in place as a fallback for a missed webhook delivery, not removed.

70 — **Session-expired redesign, super-admin INR display, and a platform-wide default-currency sweep** (`apps/tenant-web`, `apps/super-admin`, `apps/api`). Three related follow-ups.

  - **`/session-expired` redesign**: still used the older split-screen `(auth)/layout.tsx` shell (purple gradient + `StatusScreen` card) while `/login` and `/forgot-password` share a distinct premium neon-glass look — a jarring visual drop after an idle-timeout redirect. Rebuilt `SessionExpiredView` with the exact same shell/card/button markup those two pages use, and moved the route out of `(auth)` to its own top-level segment (same reason `/login`/`/forgot-password` already are — nesting a self-contained page shell under `(auth)/layout.tsx` leaves that layout's own background mounted underneath, causing a flash of the old design).
  - **Super Admin now shows every amount in INR, not USD**: per-tenant payments/invoices and plan prices already formatted using the real data's own `currency` field (INR for every seeded plan) and were already correct; only the Dashboard's Monthly/Yearly revenue and the Revenue page's MRR/ARR/per-gateway totals hardcoded `'USD'` in a local `formatMoney()`, and the Revenue page's "by currency" breakdown showed bare unformatted numbers with no symbol at all. Fixed to INR (`en-IN`) and per-row dynamic currency respectively. Also fixed a hydration-mismatch console warning (`cz-shortcut-listen`, a ColorZilla browser-extension artifact on `<body>`) — `suppressHydrationWarning` was on `<html>` but not `<body>`; `tenant-web`'s root layout already had this exact fix, super-admin's was missing it.
  - **Platform-wide default-currency sweep** ("default INR everywhere, respect an explicit choice otherwise"): `TenantSettings.currency`/`currencySymbol` and `SubscriptionPlan.currency` schema defaults were `'USD'`/`'$'` — migration `20260905082744_default_currency_inr` changes them to `'INR'`/`'₹'` (existing rows untouched, only the default for new rows). Five hardcoded `?? 'USD'`/`?? '$'` fallbacks fixed to `'INR'`/`'₹'` across `tenant.service.ts` (the `ResolvedTenant.currency` fallback — feeds almost everything), `member-payment.service.ts` (×2), `member-invoice.service.ts`, `payment.handlers.ts` (×2), `ai-context.service.ts`. **Real bug found**: the onboarding wizard lets a gym owner pick any currency (GBP/EUR/etc.) but never derived a matching symbol — `TenantSettings.currencySymbol` silently stayed at whatever the schema default was regardless of the choice. `tenant-provisioning.service.ts` now derives the real symbol via `Intl.NumberFormat(...).formatToParts()` (same mechanism `formatMoney` uses) rather than a hand-maintained map. `tenant-web`'s `PLATFORM_TENANT` placeholder also switched `USD`→`INR`. **Mobile explicitly scoped out**: `Formatters.currency()` is hardcoded to always show ₹ regardless of any tenant setting (no per-tenant dynamic currency at all, unlike web) — flagged to the user as a real but currently-inert gap (every real tenant uses INR today); user chose to leave it as-is rather than rewire the ~11 finance/reports/billing screens that call it.
  - Verified: full typecheck/eslint(0 warnings)/vitest (288/288) clean on api + tenant-web + super-admin; `Intl`-derived symbols spot-checked for all 9 onboarding-wizard currency options (INR→₹, USD→$, GBP→£, CAD→CA$, AUD→A$, AED→AED, SGD→SGD, EUR→€, NZD→NZ$).

71 — **Super Admin: "Mark Paid Manually" now captures real payment detail (mode/amount/date/proof) instead of a bare paid invoice** (`apps/api`, `apps/super-admin`). Follow-up to Prompt 8/28/29's Change Plan feature — the manual path (`AdminTenantBillingService#changePlanManually`) only ever generated+marked an Invoice PAID with no `Payment` row behind it at all, unlike a real gateway payment. Now captures the same detail a real payment carries and records a genuine `Payment` row for it.

  - **Schema**: new `PaymentGatewayProvider.MANUAL` value and a new `PaymentMode` enum (`CASH`/`BANK_TRANSFER`/`UPI`/`CHEQUE`/`CARD`/`OTHER`); `Payment` gains `paymentMode` (nullable, only set for `MANUAL`), `paidAt` (admin-entered actual payment date — may be in the past, distinct from `createdAt`), `proofUrl` (private object-storage key for an optional proof-of-payment screenshot, same `private`-visibility/`presignGetUrl()`-at-read-time convention as `Expense.receiptDataUrl`). Migration `20260905084814_manual_payment_details`.
  - **Backend**: `changePlan(planId, mode, adminUserId, adminRole, manual?)` — `mode: 'manual'` now requires a `ManualPaymentInput` (`paymentMode`, `paymentDate`, optional `amount`/`proofDataUrl`/`notes`); `changePlanManually` marks the invoice paid, uploads the proof screenshot if given (`uploadDataUrl(..., visibility: 'private')`), then creates the `Payment` row (`provider: MANUAL`, `status: SUCCEEDED`) before calling the same `applyPlanChange` tail every path already used — meaning the tenant gets the identical subscription-activated + invoice emails a real gateway payment triggers, with zero changes to that email logic. `AdminTenantBillingService#payments()` now re-signs each row's `proofUrl` at read time (same pattern as `expense.service.ts`) rather than ever returning the raw private key. New `changePlanBodySchema` (zod `.superRefine`) requires `paymentMode`/`paymentDate` only when `mode === 'manual'`.
  - **Super Admin frontend**: `ChangePlanDialog` (already shared between the Plan Detail subscriber list and — Prompt 70's other half — the Tenant Detail page) gained an inline second step: picking "Mark paid manually" now opens a form (mode-of-payment select, amount prefilled from the target plan's price, payment date, optional screenshot upload via a new `src/lib/image-to-data-url.ts` — same downscale-and-encode helper tenant-web already has, just not previously duplicated into this app — and optional notes) before submitting, instead of firing immediately with no detail captured. "Send payment link" is unchanged.
  - Verified live end-to-end against the running local stack (not a mock, no admin browser session available in this sandbox so driven directly through the service layer): switched the Wynne Meyers test tenant Professional→Enterprise via a manual `BANK_TRANSFER` entry, confirmed a real `Payment` row (`provider: MANUAL`, `paymentMode: BANK_TRANSFER`, correct `paidAt`/`amount`) and the subscription/invoice updated correctly, confirmed both the subscription-activated and invoice emails landed in Mailpit with the correct ₹17,999 Enterprise amount, then reverted back to Professional the same way (also verified) so the tenant was left in its prior state. Full typecheck/eslint(0 warnings)/vitest (288/288) clean on api + super-admin.

72 — **Member Portal: dashboard redesigned to match the staff side's data-rich home page** (`apps/tenant-web`). The member dashboard (`/portal/dashboard`) was just a profile card + membership card + QR code + a GDPR-export button — no stats, no charts, no attendance graph, no payment/classes summary, unlike the staff dashboard's KPI-row + `ChartsGrid` pattern (Prompt 27). The 8-tab shell nav (`MemberPortalShell`) already covered every module (Attendance/Workout/Diet/Measurements/Classes/Invoices) and was deliberately left alone — it's a documented, intentional simplification for the member auth plane (no RBAC, no branch switcher), not an oversight; this was purely a content gap on the one landing page.

  - Rebuilt `dashboard/page.tsx` reusing the exact same shared components the staff dashboard already uses — no new UI primitives: `StatisticCard` (4 tiles: membership days remaining, total visits, workout completion %, outstanding balance) and `ChartWrapper` (a real 30-day attendance area chart, same gradient-fill/axis-tick/tooltip styling as `ChartsGrid`'s Attendance Trends chart) — plus new "Upcoming classes" (next 7 days, filtered to the member's own `BOOKED` sessions via `useMemberBookings`) and "Recent payment history" (last 4 invoices, status-badged) cards. All from hooks/endpoints that already existed (`useMemberAttendance`, `useMemberWorkout`, `useMemberInvoices`, `useMemberClasses`, `useMemberBookings`) — zero backend changes needed. The attendance chart is built client-side (`buildAttendanceSeries`) from the flat paginated `/portal/attendance` list into a 30-bucket daily-count series, since the member-portal API has no pre-aggregated trend endpoint the way the staff-side Analytics module does.
