Mobile dashboard not redone.

107 — **Members list redesign + member reports (`apps/api`, `apps/tenant-web`)**. Design mockup approved first (same Artifact link, version 3). `(portal)/members/page.tsx` keeps every previous capability (search by name/ID/phone, status/trainer/sort filters, header-branch scoping, soft-deleted rows with Restore, per-card actions menu, select-all + bulk activate/deactivate/delete, CSV import/export, Add member, pagination, Call/WhatsApp/Renew/Punch In) and adds: `MembersHero` (face pile), `MembersKpis` (8 tiles; Total/Active/Inactive/Frozen are clickable status filters), `MembersInsights` (status donut, 6-month joinings chart, plan mix, gender, "heard about us", and a Needs-attention panel: not visited in 14 days / expiring this week / highest dues), `MembersToolbar` (sticky; status chips with live counts, trainer, sort, cards/compact-list toggle), `MemberCard` (grid + list variants: photo ring in status colour, plan chip, Due/Paid-up pill, validity bar, visits-this-month / last-visit / trainer tiles, branch, phone, actions; `Pay link` replaces Renew when money is due and links to the member page where the payment link lives) and a floating `BulkBar`. Components live in `features/members/components/list/`; the old `member-list-card.tsx` was removed. `/members` now uses the wide `PageContainer` layout (10px gutter) like the dashboard and member page. **Backend**: new `GET /members/stats` (`members:view`, optional `branchId`, honors the caller's branch access) → `MemberStatsDto` built in `MemberService#getStats` (grouped queries, no N+1; the 14-day "not visited" list reads each active member's last attendance); every list item now carries `lastCheckInAt` and `visitsThisMonth` (two grouped attendance queries per page via `MemberService#buildActivity`; detail endpoints return the defaults `null`/`0`). `formatMoney` now shows two decimals for non-integers (₹2,173.50). Verified live with 6 temporary members (statuses, dues, attendance) via Playwright at 1920 / 400px / dark — no console errors, no horizontal overflow; typecheck + eslint clean on both apps; vitest 314/314; test members removed afterwards. Not done: mobile member list, a plan filter, a branch breakdown panel.

108 — **Add Member wizard redesign — tenant-web only (`apps/tenant-web`)**. Design mockup approved first (same Artifact link, version 4). `/members/new` keeps the exact same 3-step flow, validation, field set and submit sequence (`POST /members` → `PUT /members/:id/membership` → `POST /payments`, with the Prompt 92 full-payment itemization and Prompt 97 over-payment block); only the presentation changed. New: `WizardHero` (gradient header with an animated 3-step progress line; completed steps are clickable, later ones locked until reached with Next), step transitions via `AnimatePresence`, colour-coded `PanelCard` sections (Basic details / Address / Body and goals / More details / Program), `ChipSelect` pill groups replacing the native selects for gender, goal, body type, food, marital status, awareness source and blood group, `YesNoToggle` rows for the 7 health questions (a Yes row turns red), plan cards (real discount/tax-adjusted price, tags from the plan's included PT/classes/guest passes/diet/locker/freeze, keeps the Plan details dialog), payment-mode tiles with Full / Half / Pay later quick amounts, and a sticky `WizardAside` (live member preview, required-fields completion ring + checklist, and the running proforma invoice) visible on every step. Required fields now highlight red after a failed Next. Shared pricing lives in `add-member-wizard/use-wizard-pricing.ts` so the sidebar invoice, payment step and validation can't disagree. `member-extended-info-fields.tsx` / `member-health-screening-fields.tsx` (shared with the member edit page) are untouched except that their label maps and `HEALTH_QUESTIONS` are now exported; `member-program-fields.tsx` is wizard-only and was rewritten as plan cards. `PageContainer` gives `/members/new` the full width with a 10px gutter like the other redesigned pages. Verified with Playwright: validation highlight, all three steps, a real save (gender / goal / source / blood group / a "Yes" health answer persisted, ₹100 UPI payment recorded, balance invoice ₹2,073.50 created per Prompt 105), phone width and dark mode (no overflow, no console errors); typecheck + eslint clean; the test member was removed. Mobile add-member form not redone.

109 — **Staff & Access redesign (Users / Roles / Permissions / Invitations) — `apps/tenant-web` + `apps/api`**. Design mockup approved first (same Artifact link, version 5). All four pages share `IamHero` (gradient header + tab bar with live counts; replaces the deleted `IamNav`, tabs still filtered by the same permissions). **Users**: `UsersKpis` (6 tiles; Total/Active/Pending are status filters), `UsersInsights` (status donut, people by role, last sign-in buckets, needs-attention: locked / invited-not-verified / no sign-in 30+ days), `UsersToolbar` (search, status chips with counts, role filter, cards/list toggle), `UserCard` (grid + list variants: status-ring avatar, role + 2FA tags, branch access, last sign-in dot, Email/Call/View, same actions menu as before: View/edit, Suspend, Deactivate, Restore, Delete). Import/Export/Invite/New user and the CSV logic are unchanged. **Roles**: figure tiles + colour-coded role cards (permission coverage bar vs the registry total, people count, access %, Clone / Edit / Delete exactly as permitted before — system roles stay read-only). **Permissions**: registry groups as colour-coded cards, registry/matrix toggle, matrix check marks coloured per role (still virtualized). **Invitations**: status chips and cards with a countdown ring against the 48h window; Resend/Revoke unchanged. **Backend**: new `GET /users/stats` (permission `users:read`, optional `branchId` with the same semantics as the list filter) → `UserStatsDto`; `UserListItemDto.mfaEnabled` added. User detail, new user and role form pages were not touched. The four list pages and `/members*` use the wide `PageContainer` layout (10px gutter). **No permission changes**: no keys added or removed, no role/permission seed changes. Verified: for OWNER / MANAGER / RECEPTIONIST / TRAINER the new stats endpoints return exactly the same allow/deny status as the list endpoint they sit beside (`/users` vs `/users/stats`, `/members` vs `/members/stats`); tab-count and role-card reads are now `enabled` only when the viewer holds the matching permission, so a restricted user never triggers a 403; a Playwright pass exercised search, status chips, Suspend/Restore/Deactivate/Delete+Restore, compact list, CSV export, View → untouched detail page, /users/new, role Clone + Delete, /roles/new, permissions registry + matrix, invitation Resend + Revoke (revoke refreshes the Invitations tab count); typecheck/eslint clean, vitest 314/314. Temporary test staff and invitations were removed afterwards.

  Same-session follow-up (artifact version 6): the **Invite a staff member** modal (`invite-dialog.tsx`) and the **New user** page (`users/new/page.tsx`) got the same treatment. Invite: gradient header with floating icon, email field with icon, selectable role cards (`RolePicker`, single mode; OWNER and SUPER_ADMIN still hidden), a 3-step "what happens next" strip and an animated success screen with Done; still only email + role, same `useCreateInvitation` call, same toast, Escape/Cancel close and reset it. New user: gradient hero, Account details / Temporary password (the existing `PasswordInput` + `PasswordStrengthMeter` policy checklist) / Roles (`RolePicker`, multi; OWNER offered, SUPER_ADMIN not) / Branch access (`BranchAccessCards`: same value shape as the edit page's `BranchAccessEditor`, which is untouched) cards, a sticky preview + readiness ring sidebar and a sticky Create bar; payload and validation are unchanged (the only client-side block is still "assign at least one role"; the other fields now just highlight after a failed attempt). `roleBlurb()` / `displayRoleName()` in `users-overview.tsx` give system roles readable descriptions and names everywhere (roles page, invite, create). Verified with Playwright: modal open/validation/send/success/Done/Escape, role list per flow, the invitation appears in the Invitations tab; a user created through the form had status ACTIVE, verified email, the chosen roles and primary branch, `all_branches=false`, and could log in with the temporary password; test records removed (the user's own pending invitation was left alone).

110 — **Full-width layout for Staff → Body Measurements + Payments redesign (web + API + mobile)**. (a) `PageContainer`'s `WIDE_PAGE_PATTERN` (`components/ui/page-container.tsx`) now also covers `/staff`, `/branches`, `/memberships`, `/attendance*`, `/attendance-devices`, `/workout-plans`, `/classes`, `/diet-plans`, `/measurements` and `/payments` (with their `new`/detail sub-routes): the same 10px gutter as Staff & Access instead of `max-w-7xl p-4 md:p-6 lg:p-8`. (b) **Payments redesign** (mockup approved first, Artifact "Payments Redesign"). API (`modules/finance`): new `GET /payments/analytics?dateFrom&dateTo&branchId` (`finance:view`; KPIs vs previous equal-length period, zero-filled `daily`, `methods`, `statuses`, `branches`, `topPlans`, `attention`; logic in `utils/payments-analytics.util.ts`, 11 unit tests); `GET /payments` items gained `totalRefunded`, new params `planId`/`minAmount`/`maxAmount` (also honored by the CSV/Excel exports) and a `summary:{collectedTotal,refundedTotal}` over the whole filtered set. Tenant-web: `/payments` (hero, period bar + compare toggle, KPI strip, collections chart, method donut, weekly compare, branch compare, status/refund panel, top plans, attention panel, filters bar, table), `/payments/[paymentId]` (hero, summary cards, timeline, refund-split donut, redesigned Refund dialog with quick chips/reason chips/after-status) and `/payments/new` (3-step hero, option cards, summary card) — components in `features/finance/components/payments/`, hook `usePaymentAnalytics`; `finance-badges.tsx` pills restyled app-wide. Behaviour changes on `/payments/new`: default channel is "Collected now", both notify checkboxes start unchecked and reset when the member changes. Mobile: `PaymentsAnalytics` model + `PaymentsAnalyticsCubit` + `payments_analytics_header.dart` on `PaymentsScreen`, plan/amount filters and filtered summary, refunded line on rows, refund sheet with quick chips; refund now also needs `finance:payment-refund` client-side. **Verification gaps**: API tsc/eslint clean + vitest 324/324 and live curl of the new endpoints (refund figures and previous-period values untested live — dev tenant has no refunds); tenant-web tsc/eslint clean but NOT checked in a browser (no known login — the dev tenant `kaushal-fitness` owner is `kaushalchauhan9411@gmail.com`, password unknown; the `kaushal.fitness@gmail.com` account in the credentials table no longer exists); mobile `flutter analyze` clean on touched files + 4 model tests, NOT run on a device. Dropped from web: invoice number in rows, receipt download, Columns/Density chips, lifetime-paid, outstanding sparkline.

111 — **Invoice detail page + invoice PDF redesign (`apps/tenant-web` + `apps/api`)**. Mockup approved first (Artifact "Invoice Redesign"). Web: `(portal)/invoices/[invoiceId]/page.tsx` is now a gradient hero (status badge, due date, Print / Email / Download PDF — still gated by `finance:invoice-download`, same service calls) + 4 summary cards (total, paid, balance due, due date) + Items card (totals block with notes) + Settling payments card ("Record payment" links to plain `/payments/new`, hidden when PAID/CANCELLED) + side panels (collection donut, timeline, Billed to, PDF copy). Components in `features/finance/components/invoices/`; `invoice-math.ts` derives paid = SUCCESS + PARTIALLY_REFUNDED payments (a partially-refunded payment counts at full amount — the DTO has no refunded amount), balance = max(total − paid, 0) (0 for PAID, "—" for CANCELLED). `/invoices/*` added to `WIDE_PAGE_PATTERN` (so the untouched list page is now full width too). Dropped: per-item date line, "A4" label, membership link (no backing fields). API: the PDF is now drawn by `finance/utils/invoice-pdf.renderer.ts` (+ `invoice-pdf.helpers.ts`, `invoice-logo.util.ts`), called from `MemberInvoiceService#renderPdfFor` (callers `renderPdf`/`renderOwnPdf` unchanged): gradient top bar, logo (PNG/JPEG ≤2MB fetched with a 4s timeout, else an initials tile) + legal name/address/GSTIN/contact from `TenantProfile`, meta panel, items table, payments received, notes, totals with amount paid + boxed balance due, rotated status stamp, footer from `TenantInvoiceSettings.invoiceFooter` (default thank-you line) and "Page X of Y"; page breaks repeat the table header and keep totals together. Dates are rendered "2 Oct 2026" (tenant `dateFormat` is not applied). Email still has no PDF attachment (mail infra lacks attachments); mobile never downloads the PDF, so no mobile change. Verified: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 342/342, live downloads of 3 real invoices rendered to PNG and checked (paid / unpaid), synthetic 45-item invoice paginates to 3 pages, partially-paid stamp checked after moving it clear of the amounts; **not verified**: the invoice web page in a browser (no known login), the live logo path through the API (branding is cached 300s), the member-portal download route. Design-process note: new mockups should be added as boards in the existing design artifact, not a new Artifact each time (this prompt and 110 each made a new one by mistake).

112 — **Income, Expenses and Billing redesign (web) + ledger analytics API + mobile parity**. No mockup this time — the Payments pages (Prompt 110) were the visual spec. `PageContainer`'s wide pattern now also covers `/income`, `/expenses(/new)` and `/billing/*`. **API** (`modules/finance`): new `GET /income/analytics` and `GET /expenses/analytics` (`finance:view`; same range/previous-range rules as payments analytics, reusing `utils/payments-analytics.util.ts`; logic in `utils/ledger-analytics.util.ts` + spec) returning kpis (total/count/average/largest/netProfit vs previous), zero-filled `daily`, `categories`, `branches`, `topEntries`; `GET /income` and `GET /expenses` lists gained `summary:{total,count,average}` over the whole filtered set. `branches[]` skips entries with no branch (can sum to less than `total` without a branch filter); `description` can be null. **Tenant-web**: `/income`, `/expenses`, `/expenses/new` rebuilt with shared building blocks in `features/finance/components/ledger/` (KPI strip, trend chart, category donut, branch compare, top entries, filters bar, table, chip-based Add-income dialog, expense summary card), hooks `useIncomeAnalytics`/`useExpenseAnalytics`, `INCOME/EXPENSE_CATEGORY_META` + `CategoryBadge` in finance-badges; period/branch/date now drive both analytics and the list; CSV/Excel export moved into the hero; analytics section hidden without `finance:view`. Dropped: amount-range filter (list API has no such params), net-profit sparkline (no daily net series), `IncomeFormFields` is no longer rendered (dialog has its own markup). `/billing`, `/billing/history`, `/billing/invoices`, `/billing/address` rebuilt around `features/billing/components/billing-hero.tsx` (hero + Overview/History/Invoices/Address tabs; `billing-nav.tsx` deleted), plan cards, a monthly spend chart derived client-side, history/invoice tables with status chips, and a live invoice-preview card on the address form. Checkout/verify/Razorpay logic and hooks untouched; cancel mutation + confirm moved into `billing/page.tsx`. Billing dropped: payment-method/card details, invoice line items / PDF preview (no backing data); "next amount" is derived (plan price × cycle). History/invoice summaries use the first row's currency. **Mobile**: `LedgerAnalytics` model, `LedgerAnalyticsCubit`, `ledger_analytics_header.dart` + shared `analytics_parts.dart` (also used by the payments header) on the Income and Expense list screens, plus a summary line; `IncomeRepository`/`ExpenseRepository` gained `analytics()` and `listWithSummary()`. Billing is web-only. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 350/350, live curl of both endpoints with throwaway rows (inserted and deleted; counts restored) covering previous-period figures, netProfit, branch filter, inverted range, foreign branch; web tsc/eslint clean (re-run by me); mobile `flutter analyze` clean on touched files + 7 tests. **Not verified**: every web page in a browser (no known login for `kaushal-fitness`, whose owner is `kaushalchauhan9411@gmail.com`), Razorpay checkout / cancel / invoice download flows, mobile on a device, and the mobile parsing against real API JSON (only hand-written test fixtures). Note: a prettier pass by the API agent left whitespace churn in `member-invoice.service.ts`, `member-payment.service.ts`, `member-payment.repository.ts` — check `git diff` before committing.

113 — **Reports redesign — hub, report viewer, Analytics (web) + reports overview/summary API + mobile parity**. No mockup (user: "design like professional with animation and transition with good color", "add charts and graphs, comparison etc"); Payments/Ledger/Billing were the visual family. `PageContainer`'s wide pattern now also covers `/reports(/x)` and `/analytics`. **API** (`modules/reports`): new `GET /reports/overview?dateFrom&dateTo&branchId` (`reports:view`; kpis vs previous equal-length period, zero-filled `daily` with `prev*` fields, `weekdayAttendance`, `hourlyAttendance`, `memberStatus`, `planDistribution`, `paymentMethods`, `branches`, `topTrainers`, `expiringBuckets`; `utils/reports-overview.util.ts`, `services/reports-overview.service.ts`) and `GET /reports/:type/summary` for all 11 report types (generic `kpis[]` / `breakdowns[]` / `series` shape; `utils/report-summary.util.ts`, `services/report-summary.service.ts`); `GET /analytics/branch-comparison` and its export now accept optional `dateFrom`/`dateTo`. Definitions: churn = membership EXPIRED with endDate in range or CANCELLED with updatedAt in range (no `cancelledAt` column); overview revenue = SUCCESS + PARTIALLY_REFUNDED finalAmount while `/reports/revenue/summary` counts SUCCESS only (can differ); hour = UTC hour of `checkInTime`, weekday = UTC weekday of `attendanceDate` (0 = Sunday); new members by `Member.createdAt`; member-progress summary reads at most 2000 members. **Tenant-web**: shared foundation in `features/reports/{lib,components/ui,charts}` (motion variants + `useMotionSafe`, `reports-theme.ts` per-category accents, `ReportsHero`, `KpiTile`, `ChartCard`, `AnimatedNumber`, `Reveal`/`StaggerGroup`, Trend/MultiSeries/Donut/HBar/GroupedBar/Heatmap/RadialGauge charts, `report-catalog.ts`, `useReportsControls`, `useReportsOverview`, `useReportSummary`); `/reports` = hero + period bar with compare + 9 KPI tiles + trend/weekday/peak-hours/status/plan/method/branch/trainer/expiring charts + searchable category-filtered catalogue + restyled scheduled-reports panel (logic unchanged); `/reports/[reportType]` = hero, summary KPI strip, generic breakdown/series charts, filters (chips/dates/branch/plan/status where the list API supports them), animated table card; `/analytics` = animated tabbed deep-dive (`?view=<slug>`) with this-vs-previous overlays, branch comparison as bars, "Invoiced" (not "expenses") for payment collection, retention labelled "Active members (daily)", exports in focus mode. Behaviour changes: date-range reports and Analytics now default to "This month" (was empty / last 30 days); the branch-comparison pie was dropped; hero "Schedule report" only scrolls to the panel. **Mobile**: `ReportsOverview`/`ReportSummary`/`BranchComparisonRow` models, `ReportsOverviewCubit`, `ReportSummaryCubit`, `reports_insights_header.dart` on the Reports Center (pull-to-refresh), `ReportSummaryHeader` on all 11 report screens, `lib/shared/widgets/motion.dart` (no new package, honours `disableAnimations`), Revenue Report "By branch" follows the date range, new "Revenue by branch" card on Analytics. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 378/378, live curl of overview + all 11 summaries + branch-comparison with throwaway rows (inserted and deleted; counts restored); web tsc/eslint clean (re-run by me after both page agents finished); mobile `flutter analyze` clean on touched files + 18 tests (fixtures derived from the backend code, widget tests at 360px). **Not verified**: every web page in a browser — animations, dark mode, responsive stacking, print layout (no known login for `kaushal-fitness`; owner `kaushalchauhan9411@gmail.com`); mobile on a device and against the live API; restricted-branch/non-owner roles on the new endpoints; trainer/staff summaries with real data (dev tenant has none); percent-KPI scale on mobile is inferred (payments success-rate is 0..1, member-progress averages 0..100).

114 — **Notifications + Announcements redesign (web) + stats API + mobile parity**. No mockup; the Reports foundation kit (`features/reports/{lib,components/ui,charts}`) and the Payments/Billing pages were the visual family. `PageContainer`'s wide pattern now also covers `/notifications(/x)` and `/announcements(/x)`. **API**: new `GET /notifications/stats` (`notifications:view`: kpis total/read/readRate vs previous + current unread, zero-filled `daily`, `categories` with previous counts, 24-row `hourly` — UTC hours) and `GET /tenant-announcements/stats` (`announcements:view`: published vs previous, current drafts/scheduled/expired, expiringSoon (next 7 days), `daily`, `byStatus`, `byAudience`, `byBranch`, `upcoming`, `expiring`, `recent`); `GET /notifications` gained `category`/`search` params and `counts:{all,unread}`; `GET /tenant-announcements` gained `audience`/`search` and `counts:{all,draft,scheduled,published,expired}`; `unreadOnly=false` is now parsed as false (it was truthy via `z.coerce.boolean`). Logic in `tenant-notifications/utils/notification-stats.util.ts` and `tenant-announcements/utils/announcement-stats.util.ts` (+ specs); `isoDay` is now exported from `finance/validators/finance.validators.ts`. **Known gap**: `recent[].delivered`/`read` are always null — `MemberNotification` has no link to the announcement that produced it (`notifyMembersInTenant` sets no `sourceNotificationId`), so per-announcement reach cannot be derived without guessing; UIs hide the bars. Staff-feed `readAt` is tenant-wide (one value shared by all staff), so UIs word it "read by your team". **Tenant-web**: `/notifications` = hero + period bar + KPI tiles + volume trend (received vs read toggle), read-rate gauge, category donut, hourly heatmap + server-filtered inbox (category chips, Unread/All with counts, debounced server search, day-grouped animated feed); `NotificationFeed` is shared with the header bell drawer (`compact` still works; new optional props showTabs/grouped/canDelete/emptyTitle/emptyDescription) and the bell panel got a gradient header + animated badge; `/notifications/settings` = template cards by purpose with channel chips, Active switch (new), restyled edit dialog; templates are only fetched with `notifications:manage` (previously 403'd for others); delete hidden without `notifications:manage`; realtime `notification:new` now also invalidates `['tenant-announcements']`. `/announcements` = hero + 5 KPI tiles + publish trend, status donut, audience compare, branch bars, upcoming/expiring countdown lists, recent-reach list + status tabs with server counts, audience chips, debounced search, animated card grid (page size 12), restyled form dialog (with live preview) and schedule dialog (1 hour / tomorrow 9am / custom presets); trusted-HTML body rendering and all permission/status gating unchanged. Fixed: the old Unread tab and search only filtered the loaded page. Caveat: the hourly heatmap/busiest-hour show UTC hours without saying so (a gym in IST sees peaks ~5.5h off). **Mobile**: `NotificationStats`/`AnnouncementStats` models, generic `PeriodStatsCubit`, `FilterChipsRow`/`DebouncedSearchField` (`shared/widgets/list_filters.dart`), insights headers on the Notifications and Announcements screens with server-side filters + load-more, new search box on Notifications; member plane untouched. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 388/388, live curl of both stats endpoints and both lists with throwaway rows (inserted and deleted; counts restored); web tsc/eslint clean (re-run by me); mobile `flutter analyze` clean on touched files (3 known pre-existing issues) + 28 tests (models checked against fixtures from the backend code; no live API call, no device). **Not verified**: every web page in a browser (no known login for `kaushal-fitness`; owner `kaushalchauhan9411@gmail.com`), mobile on a device, non-owner/permission-denied paths, multi-branch tenants, large feeds.

115 — **Administration redesign: Gym Settings, Support, account Settings (web) + support stats API + mobile Support parity**. No mockup (user: no artifacts). `PageContainer`'s wide pattern now also covers `/gym-settings/*`, `/support` and `/settings`. **API**: new `GET /support/tickets/stats` (`support:view`: created vs previous, current open/inProgress/resolved/closed/unresolved, `oldestOpenDays`, zero-filled `daily`, `byStatus`, `byPriority` this vs previous; `support/utils/ticket-stats.util.ts` + spec) and `GET /support/tickets` gained `counts:{all,open,inProgress,resolved,closed}` (ignores the status filter). Status/priority counts are by UTC `createdAt` day; there is no category/SLA/resolvedAt, so no response-time metrics. **Tenant-web / Gym Settings** (`features/gym-settings`, presentation only — no behaviour change): all six pages (profile, business, branding, invoice, security, ai) share `SettingsHero` (hero + route tab bar; `GymSettingsNav` deleted), `SettingsLayout`, colour-coded `SectionCard`s, a restyled `UnsavedChangesBar`, and real-data hero stats; a `SetupProgress` gauge/checklist (profile fields %, branding assets x/5, hours days x/7, MFA roles x/4) and live previews built from form state (`previews.tsx`: branding login/sidebar preview, invoice header preview, business Intl date/time/currency preview; profile weekly-hours bars + contact card; security role cards + static "how 2FA works"; AI key saved/not-saved chip — the key is never shown — and a temperature gauge; the AI provider `<select>` became a card radiogroup on the same form value). The `useState` + baseline-diff + `UnsavedChangesBar` form mechanism, hooks, `settings:manage`/`settings:read` gates, branding `refreshPortalChrome()` chain and `silent: true` uploads are untouched. **Support** (`features/support`): hero + period bar, KPI tiles (created w/ delta, unresolved, open, resolved, oldest-open age turning orange at ≥7 days), created-per-day this-vs-previous, status donut, priority grouped bars, help-centre cards + FAQ accordion + support-email card, tickets table with server status counts, restyled New-ticket / Ticket-detail (notes timeline) / Contact dialogs. Behaviour changes: `NewTicketDialog` is now controlled (page owns the gated "New ticket" buttons); the ticket list only queries when the plan flag and `support:view` allow; hero "Contact us" is a mailto link. **Account Settings** (`features/account-settings`, `/settings`): shared hero with the five tabs (`?tab=` still seeds the tab; tab change calls `router.replace`), hero stats = 2FA on/off, active-session count, last sign-in; Account / Password (the unchanged `ChangePasswordForm` already has a strength meter) / Security / Notifications / Sessions tabs restyled. The Notifications tab is now an honest read-only preview labelled "not saved" with a link to `/profile` (the real preferences); Sessions keeps revoke and sign-out-everywhere unchanged and shows no location (the API has none). The 2FA dialogs in `features/auth` were deliberately NOT restyled (mount-effect `useMutation` trap — setup flow untouched). **Mobile**: `SupportTicketStats` model, `SupportInsightsHeader` (+ `SupportStatsCubit` = generic `PeriodStatsCubit`), status `FilterChipsRow` with server counts on the Support tickets screen, repository `stats()`/`listWithCounts()`; app-bar "N open" is now open + in-progress; Created delta is colour-inverted (a rise shows red — agent's call, easy to change); gym-settings/settings stay web-only. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 395/395, live curl of the stats endpoint + list counts with throwaway tickets (inserted and deleted; counts restored); web tsc/eslint clean (re-run by me); mobile `flutter analyze` clean on touched files (3 known pre-existing issues) + 33 tests (model validated against fixtures from the backend code; no live API call, no device). Process note: the first Gym Settings agent died on an API spend-limit error after writing the shared pieces + Profile page; a second agent finished the other five pages. **Not verified**: every web page in a browser (no known login for `kaushal-fitness`; owner `kaushalchauhan9411@gmail.com`) — notably the branding live-update chain, uploads, the `?tab=` replace behaviour, ticket dialogs, animations, dark mode; mobile on a device; a populated `closed` ticket count; non-owner permission paths.

116 — **Invoices list + Generate invoice, profile dropdown + My Profile, notification bell panel (web) + invoice analytics API + mobile Invoices parity**. No mockups (user: no artifacts). `PageContainer`'s wide pattern now also covers `/profile` (and already `/invoices/*`). **API** (`modules/finance`): new `GET /invoices/analytics` (`finance:invoice-view`; invoiced/count/collected/avgInvoice/collectionRate vs previous, current outstanding + overdue, zero-filled `daily`, `byStatus` (all five), receivables `aging` buckets 'Current','1-30','31-60','61-90','90+', `topDebtors`, `branches`; `utils/invoice-analytics.util.ts` + spec; five `$queryRaw` aggregates in `repository.analyticsRaw`) and `GET /invoices` gained `minAmount`/`maxAmount`, `summary:{invoiced,collected,outstanding,count}` and `counts:{all,unpaid,partiallyPaid,paid,overdue,cancelled}`. Definitions: payment↔invoice link is `MemberPayment.invoiceId`; collected = SUCCESS + PARTIALLY_REFUNDED `finalAmount` linked to non-CANCELLED invoices dated in range; **OVERDUE is derived, not stored** (nothing writes it): effective OVERDUE = stored OVERDUE or UNPAID/PARTIALLY_PAID with `dueDate < today (UTC)`; the `status` filter, `counts` and `byStatus` use the effective status while each item's own `status` stays the stored value (so a past-due row can read UNPAID); outstanding/overdue/aging/topDebtors ignore the range. **Tenant-web**: `/invoices` = hero + period bar/compare + KPI tiles (invoiced, collected, collection-rate gauge, outstanding, overdue, avg) + invoiced-over-time this vs previous, status donut, ageing bars, top debtors (→ `/members/<id>`), branch bars (hidden for <2 branches) + status chips with server counts, date + amount range, table with due-in/overdue chips and View/Download/Email row actions (gated `finance:invoice-download`), footer summary + pagination; behaviour changes: list defaults to **This month** (older invoices need a wider period), status is chips not a select, branch scoping comes from the Reports controls (seeded from the header branch, plus 'All branches'). `/invoices/new` = hero + step cards + live summary (sticky on wide screens) + due-date presets (+7/15/30 days that only fill the existing field); fields/validation/`useGenerateInvoice` unchanged. Components in `features/finance/components/invoices/`; hook `useInvoiceAnalytics`. Dropped: collected series in the trend (no daily collected field), CSV/Excel export (none exists). **Profile menu + My Profile**: `components/layout/profile-menu.tsx` is a gradient-header menu (initials ring, role chips from `useCurrentUser().roles`, tenant name, icon-chip items with subtitles, staggered open, `w-72 max-w-[calc(100vw-1rem)]` + `collisionPadding` so it no longer clips); 'Notification Settings' now links to `/profile#notifications` (the profile page scrolls to it on load and on `hashchange`; the old `/settings?tab=notifications` is a read-only 'not saved' preview); `/profile` = hero (photo/initials, role + branch chips, completeness/created/last sign-in/2FA stats), colour-coded panels, animated `role="switch"` preference toggles (still save instantly, revert on error), completeness ring from real saved fields only, quick links (password, security, sessions, help); no `UnsavedChangesBar` (page never had dirty-state). **Bell panel**: `components/layout/notification-panel.tsx` + `features/notifications/components/bell-panel/{bell-button,bell-panel-feed}.tsx` — bell rings once when Redux `unreadCount` rises, badge caps at 99+, pings only while unread>0; 420px right Drawer with gradient header ('read by your team'), Unread/All tabs on the server `unreadOnly` param + list `counts`, day-grouped feed with category chips, hover mark-read/delete (delete needs `notifications:manage`), 'Load more' raises `limit` by 20, footer 'View all'; the shared `NotificationFeed` and the `/notifications` page are unchanged (the bell no longer uses its compact props). **Mobile**: `InvoiceAnalytics` model, `InvoiceInsightsHeader` (reuses `PeriodStatsCubit`), `invoices_screen.dart` rebuilt as a `CustomScrollView` with insights, debounced server search, status chips with counts, filtered summary line, load-more; `InvoiceRepository.list()` unchanged (Dashboard Outstanding sheet uses it); member-portal invoice screens untouched. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 418/418, live curl with throwaway invoices/items/payments (inserted and deleted; counts restored); web tsc/eslint clean per agent (whole app); mobile `flutter analyze` clean on touched files (3 known pre-existing issues) + 38 tests (model checked field-by-field against the landed backend DTO; no live call, no device). **Not verified**: every page in a browser for these three pieces (the staff-account browser check was still running when this was written), mobile on a device, branch-restricted/non-owner actors and the `finance:invoice-view` 403 path, Download/Email row actions, the bell's focus trap/Esc. **Dev-server note**: during the invoice API work the API's `tsx watch` stopped reloading and the agent's restart killed it; it was relaunched detached with nohup (log in the session scratchpad `api.log`) — restart `pnpm dev` in `apps/api` from your own terminal if you want logs back.

117 — **Staff role audit + test accounts (no app code changed)**. Created Manager/Trainer/Receptionist test logins (credentials table above) and ran a static audit plus a live Playwright/API check as each role. Finding: the redesigned pages are NOT owner-only — they live in the shared staff `(portal)` layout and are gated purely by permission keys (OWNER `*`, MANAGER 116 keys, TRAINER 36, RECEPTIONIST 43). Confirmed per-role API status (manager / trainer / receptionist): finance list+analytics endpoints 200 / 403 / 200; settings/subscription/payment-history 200 / 403 / 403; reports overview, notifications+stats, announcements+stats, support+stats 200 for all. Open issues (unfixed, ranked): (1) financial data reaches roles that shouldn't see it — receptionist (`finance:view`) gets income/expenses lists + analytics + Net profit, trainer (`reports:view`) sees revenue/net profit on `/reports` and `/reports/revenue/summary`; `reports:view-financial` and `finance:expense-manage` aren't enforced on those reads; (2) `/gym-settings/*` shows a blank skeleton forever for roles without `settings:read` (403s, no denied state); (3) no route-level permission gate — hidden pages open by URL and show per-widget "Missing required permission" panels instead of a clean no-access page (`RequirePermission` exists in `features/auth/guards/` but isn't used in the portal pages); (4) the layout shell calls `/settings/business` on every page (console 403 for trainer/receptionist) and the dashboard calls `/analytics/revenue-trends` + `/onboarding-checklist` without a permission check; (5) horizontal overflow at 1024px on `/invoices`, `/reports/revenue`, `/announcements`; (6) `/public/app-releases/latest` 404 on every page (dev-only update check); (7) restricted-branch users: the branch switcher lists all branches and defaults to the tenant default, so reports/analytics 403 and payments/income return empty; (8) payments analytics isn't hidden on error (income/expenses are), billing Cancel buttons aren't permission-gated, the account `/settings` nav item needs `settings:manage` so trainer/receptionist lose the link to change their own password. Manager renders every redesigned page with real data and the 10px gutter. Not verified: most 1024px screenshots were checked by scripted status/overflow only; the receptionist `/reports/revenue` and `/settings` captures timed out (dev server slowness, not retried).

118 — **Member portal complete redesign (web `/portal/*` + member-plane API + mobile member app)**. User: "completely redesign with good animation and transition with good colors with more details visible", phone-first. No mockups. **API** (`modules/member-portal`, member token, all scoped to the authenticated member): new `GET /portal/overview` (membership with daysLeft/totalDays/price/amountPaid, attendance thisMonth vs previous + current/best streak + 7 weekday rows + 84-day `daily` + avgVisitMinutes, workout/diet progress, billing outstanding/nextDue/paidLast90Days, next 3 booked classes, unread count; `utils/member-overview.util.ts` + spec), `GET /portal/payments` (own payments incl. invoice number + refunded), `GET /portal/invoices/:id` (own invoice detail with items, payments, paid, balance), `GET /portal/gym` (public-safe gym info only — never GST/registration/internal settings; mostly null on this tenant because its profile is empty); `GET /portal/notifications` gained `unreadOnly` + `category` (and `unreadOnly=false` is now parsed correctly). **Security fix**: `MemberInvoiceService.getOwnById`/`renderOwnPdf` were not scoped to the member inside the service (the controller checked ownership, but any new caller would have leaked) — now `MemberInvoiceRepository.findOwnById(tenantId, memberId, id)` puts the member in the WHERE and foreign ids 404; the other member-plane actions (pay checkout/verify, booking cancel, workout/diet progress, notification read) were already safe; `member-portal.isolation.spec.ts` (7 tests) guards it. Definitions: streak = consecutive UTC days with a visit ending today/yesterday; previous = prior month 1st→same day; 84-day window drives weekday/daily/avgVisitMinutes while totalVisits is all-time; membership pick ACTIVE → soonest PENDING → latest EXPIRED; price = `priceAtAssignment` + current plan discount then tax; amountPaid = settled payments linked by membership id or via its invoices, counted once. **Tenant-web**: new shell (`features/member-portal/components/shell/*`, single `PORTAL_NAV` array in `lib/nav.ts` — append one entry to add a page): phone = slim top bar with real unread bell + fixed bottom tab bar (Home, Attendance, Workout, Classes) + 'More' sheet; desktop = grouped left rail (Overview / Training / Account); tenant-primary gradients via `color-mix`; enter-only page transitions. Portal kit `features/member-portal/components/kit` (PortalHero, StatTile, SectionCard, ListRow/StatusChip, ProgressRing, AnimatedNumber, PortalHeatmap, WeekdayBars, SheetModal, …) — portal-local money (`usePortalMoney()` from `useTenant().currency`) because the staff `useCurrencySymbol` hook calls the staff `apiClient` and must never be used in the portal. Pages: Dashboard (hero + days-left ring, 6 tiles, 12-week heatmap, upcoming classes, workout/body snapshot, recent invoices w/ Pay now, gym card; falls back to per-resource hooks if overview errors), Profile, Attendance, Workout (Mon–Sun day-picker, Complete/Skip), Diet (meal cards, water stepper, weight, trends), Measurements (computed BMI labelled, range charts, first-vs-latest), Classes (week strip, capacity bars, cancel now confirms in a sheet), Renew (Razorpay flow unchanged; button disabled until the plan has expired), Invoices + NEW `/portal/invoices/[id]` detail, NEW `/portal/payments`, NEW `/portal/gym`, Notifications (server filters, day-grouped), and the public activate/reset-password pages restyled with tenant branding (logic untouched). Behaviour changes: Renew disabled until expiry (assumes the backend rejects early renewal — consistent with Prompt 93, not re-checked); Book hidden for ended sessions; Pay hidden on cancelled invoices; 'load more' on invoices/payments/notifications raises `limit`; invoice status chips and payments 'Paid (loaded)' count loaded rows only (list endpoints take only page/limit). Dropped (no backing data): sets/reps/rest per exercise, foods/macros per meal, water goal, trainer contact/photo, member photo on mobile, guest passes/referrals/goals. **Mobile** (`features/member/`): `MemberOverview`/`PortalPayment`/`MemberInvoiceDetail`/`GymInfo` models; dashboard 'Insights' section (`PeriodStatsCubit<MemberOverview>`, hides if overview errors), enriched invoice detail (discount/paid/balance/payments), new Payments and Gym info screens (menu + routes), Notifications filter chips with server params. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 445/445, live curl as MEM-0001 incl. foreign-id 404s; web tsc/eslint clean (re-run by me after all agents finished); **real browser check as MEM-0001 at 390×844 and 1280×800 by the agents** (first real rendering of any redesign this session): every built page rendered, no horizontal scroll, only `/public/app-releases/latest` 404s and login rate-limit 429s; mobile `flutter analyze` 2 pre-existing issues (branch_hours_screen, member_visit_detail_screen) + 52 tests, models validated against REAL JSON from MEM-0001's login (no device run). Throwaway ZZ-THROWAWAY rows (attendance, measurements, workout/diet plans, classes/sessions/booking) were inserted for the browser checks and deleted — counts verified back to baseline. **Not verified**: Razorpay pay/renew (keys blank in dev), clicking Complete/Skip/Done/Save, class book/cancel in the browser, the reset-password/activate form states, dark mode, final cosmetic tweaks made after the last screenshots (invoice-detail ring text, payments hero label, attendance row layout, biceps colour), invoice list with an unpaid/overdue invoice (MEM-0001's are all paid), real gym info (tenant profile empty), mobile on a device. Follow-up (same day): the shell content wrapper (`member-portal-shell.tsx`) no longer caps width at 1200px — it is now `w-full px-2.5` (10px gutter, like the staff pages), so the portal fills the screen right of the desktop rail; verified at 1920/1280/390 with no horizontal scroll (on very wide screens the heatmap cells and cards stretch). Dev note: the shared `/login` rate-limits repeated logins (429) — wait it out when scripting.

119 — **Member self-service profile editing with photo (API + web `/portal/profile` + mobile member app)**. User request: members update their own profile and photo. This deliberately reverses the old rule that member profile fields were staff-managed (only password change was member-writable). **API** (`modules/member-portal`, member token; member id taken only from the token): `GET /portal/profile` (full DTO incl. `locked{branch,trainer,status,joiningDate,membership}` and `editable[]` key list; `GET /portal/me` unchanged), `PATCH /portal/profile` (flat optional keys, strict zod `.strict()` whitelist — unknown/locked keys → 422; `""`/`null` clears optional fields; unchanged values are no-ops), `POST /portal/profile/photo` (`{image: data URL}`, JPEG/PNG/WebP by magic bytes, decoded cap ≈ 740 KB because the app JSON limit is 1 MB; 413 if larger), `DELETE /portal/profile/photo`. Editable: firstName, lastName, email, phone, dateOfBirth, gender, address (line/city/state/country/postal), emergency contact (name/phone/relation), occupation, height, weight, bloodGroup, maritalStatus, anniversary, goal, bodyType, foodPreference, fitnessGoals. Locked/read-only: memberId, status, joiningDate, branch, trainer, membership, QR, biometricId, referredBy, notes, registrationFee; not exposed or editable: health-screening answers, medicalConditions, allergies, fatherNameOrAadhaar, awarenessSource. **Email change requires `currentPassword`** only when the email actually changes (wrong/missing → 422 on `currentPassword`, and a wrong password counts toward the failed-login lockout → 423); duplicate email/phone → 409 with a generic message; the previous address gets a best-effort 'email changed' notice (`memberEmailChangedEmail`, only queued — delivery not verified); login identity stays the member ID. Writes limited to 30 per 15 min per member (bypassed under `NODE_ENV=test`). Photos stored under `public/member-photos/<tenantId>/<memberId>/`; replacing/removing deletes the old object (local-disk path verified, S3 not). Audit: `actorUserId:null, actorRole:'MEMBER'`, actions `member_portal.profile_updated` (changed field NAMES only; old→new only for goal/bodyType/foodPreference/maritalStatus), `member_portal.photo_updated`, `member_portal.photo_removed`, `member_auth.reauth_failed`; `AuditLogRepository#record` gained an optional `after` JSON. Shared code touched: `MemberAuthService#verifyCurrentPassword`, `deleteStoredMemberPhoto` in `core/storage/storage.service.ts`, a 413 mapping in `error-handler.middleware.ts`. A wrong current password returns 422 (not 401) so clients don't treat the session as expired. **Tenant-web** (`features/member-portal/components/profile/*`, `services/member-profile.service.ts`, hooks `useMemberSelfProfile`/`useUpdateMemberProfile`/`useUploadMemberPhoto`/`useRemoveMemberPhoto` — the name `useMemberProfile` already means GET /portal/me; `memberProfileUpdated` reducer keeps the Redux member name/email in sync): hero with avatar + camera button + completeness ring (real DTO fields only), editable cards (Personal / Contact / Address / Emergency contact / Fitness profile; one column on phones, two from md), a 'Managed by your gym' locked card, security card (change-password form behind an expand button), fixed bottom Save/Discard bar above the phone tab bar, only changed keys PATCHed, `beforeunload` warning, 422 field errors inline, 409/413/429 messages; photo pipeline = center-crop square → max 512px → JPEG q0.85, stepping down to q0.35 until the data URL < 900k chars, with a 'Take photo' option on phones (`capture`). **Mobile** (`features/member/`): `MemberSelfProfile` + `member_profile_form.dart` (option lists, changed-fields diff, validator), `ProfileFailure`, `profile_photo.dart` (magic-byte sniff, 900,000-char cap), `member_edit_profile_screen.dart`, `member_photo_sheet.dart`, rewritten `member_profile_screen.dart`; the member session now carries the photo URL (`SessionCubit.memberProfileChanged`; `SessionAuthenticatedMember.props` had to include it or Equatable swallowed the emit); the photo is downscaled by `image_picker`'s own `maxWidth/maxHeight: 512, imageQuality: 85` (no new dependency — there is no client-side square crop; the circular `BoxFit.cover` avatar looks cropped); option lists mirror the backend zod enums because the staff mobile form uses free text; the QR the endpoint returns is not shown (no surface in the member app). **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 479/479 (42 new); live curl as MEM-0001 (locked-field attempts → 422, email change with no/wrong/correct password, SVG-as-PNG → 422, 900 KB body → 413, photo replace/remove) and **real browser check on web at 390×844 and 1280×800** (edit + save, photo upload updating avatar in hero/top bar/rail, remove, inline email-password errors); mobile `flutter analyze` only the 2 known pre-existing infos + 69 tests (model validated against a sanitised REAL response; widget tests at 360px). MEM-0001's real record was snapshotted before and restored after both the API and web tests (members row identical to the snapshot via psql; original photo file restored with matching sha256; no password/email changed) — re-checked by me: login works, photo present, failed attempts 0. The append-only audit log keeps the test entries. **Not verified**: mobile on a device (real camera/gallery picking, permissions, keyboard-safe scrolling, upload against the live server), dark mode, HEIC/real phone-camera files, the 429 rate-limit message, delivery of the old-address email notice, S3 photo deletion, the unsaved-changes dialog on mobile.

120 — **[SUPERSEDED by 122: the JARVIS HUD front end was rejected by the user ("too much jarvis") and fully removed; the admin-plane API half of this entry — overview endpoint, tenant controls, definitions — is still current]** Super admin dashboard → "Iron Man JARVIS HUD" (apps/super-admin + admin-plane API). User brief: JARVIS-style, colourful, strong animation/transitions, more detail + control over tenants, a theme deliberately different from the tenant and member apps; "first update the super admin dashboard, then the next pages after approval"; free hand on the super-admin web side (no mobile app) as long as tenant/member apps and their APIs are not broken (tenant-web/mobile untouched; tenant-web `tsc` re-run clean; API 497/497 tests). **API** (`modules/admin-dashboard`, `admin-tenants`): new `GET /admin/dashboard/overview?range=7d|30d|90d` (`dashboard:read`; kpis with previous-period values, zero-filled `signupsDaily`/`revenueDaily`, `planMix`, `countries`, `supportTickets`, `trialsExpiring`, `atRisk`, `topTenants`, `activity` feed with a human `summary`, `health` {database, redis, queue, uptime}); invalid range → 422. Permission-scoped fields degrade instead of 403: no `payments:read` → `revenueCollected`/`failedPayments` null + `revenueDaily` []; no `revenue:read` → `mrr`/`arr` null + `planMix` []; no `support:manage` → `supportTickets` null; no `scheduler:view` → `health.queue` null (these degrade paths are unit-level only — only the SUPER_ADMIN login was available). Definitions: **MRR = monthly-normalised price (yearly/12) of ACTIVE subscriptions of non-deleted tenants only — TRIALING excluded, so it is lower than `/admin/revenue/summary`, which still counts trials**; `mrr.previous` is always null (no historical snapshots); churned = distinct tenants with a CANCELED/EXPIRED subscription whose `COALESCE(cancelled_at, updated_at)` is in the window (EXPIRED uses `updated_at` as a proxy); trialConversion = share of subscriptions whose trial ended in the window that are ACTIVE now (null if none ended); atRisk precedence PAYMENT_FAILED > GRACE > PAST_DUE > SUSPENDED_RECENTLY > NEAR_LIMIT (usage ≥ 90% of limit), one entry per tenant, max 10; topTenants by the `members` usage metric (fallback newest-first with members null); revenue is summed across currencies without conversion (same as the existing revenue code). New tenant controls (`tenants:manage`, each writes an admin audit row `admin.tenant_trial_extended` / `admin.tenant_maintenance_changed` / `admin.tenant_force_logout` and invalidates the tenant cache): `POST /admin/tenants/:id/extend-trial {days 1..90, reason?}` (TRIAL tenants only; extends tenant + TRIALING subscription from the later of now/current end), `POST .../maintenance {enabled, reason?}` (409 if already in that state; **enforced**: `tenantService.assertTenantAccessible` via `tenantMiddleware` returns 503 `MAINTENANCE` on every tenant-plane request incl. login), `POST .../force-logout` (revokes all refresh tokens + user sessions; **already-issued access tokens keep working until their short TTL** because the tenant authenticate middleware checks only the jti denylist). `admin-auth/middlewares/admin-authorize.middleware.ts` gained an exported `getAdminPermissions()` (`requireAdminPermission` behaviour unchanged). No schema/migration changes. **Super-admin web**: `features/dashboard/components/hud/*` (24 files): scoped HUD theme (`hud-theme.css`, imported once from globals.css; global `:root`/`.dark` tokens untouched), fonts via `next/font/google` (Orbitron headings, Rajdhani UI/tiles, JetBrains Mono telemetry — self-hosted at build, CSP-safe; KPI tiles use Rajdhani because Orbitron's slashed zero looked broken), arc-reactor 'Platform Pulse' (rotating rings, outer arcs = real tenant-status proportions, core = total tenants + PLATFORM ONLINE/DEGRADED from `health`), status strip (local+UTC clocks, API uptime, LIVE ring counting down to the 45 s refresh, 7D/30D/90D selector), 11 KPI holo-tiles (animated tickers, ▲/▼ with inverted colouring for failed payments/churn, sparklines, scan sweep), neon charts (signups this vs previous, daily revenue, plan-mix donut, country bars, support-ticket ring, DB/Redis pulse lines + queue chip), intel panels (trials expiring with inline Extend, at-risk tenants, top tenants), streaming-terminal activity feed, backdrop (grid, hex, scanlines, vignette, ≤48-particle canvas paused when hidden / off under reduced motion), boot sequence once per browser session (skippable; skipped under reduced motion), and a tenant COMMAND CONSOLE: debounced search (`GET /admin/tenants?search=`) or click any tenant row → side drawer (bottom sheet <1024px) with status/plan/subscription/trial/owner/usage/maintenance and actions suspend/reactivate, extend trial, maintenance toggle, force logout, impersonate (opens the returned portalUrl after confirm), reset owner password, link to the full tenant page; every action behind a HUD confirmation dialog (suspend, force logout, reset password and maintenance-on require typing the slug); gated by `useHasPermission('tenants:read'/'tenants:manage')`; delete is deliberately NOT exposed; no mutation fires from a mount effect. Shell integration: `(portal)/layout.tsx` sets `data-hud="true"` on the shell root only while on `/dashboard` (scoped CSS darkens sidebar/header/Sign out and zeroes `<main>` padding); `/tenants` etc. verified unchanged after navigating away. If the overview call fails, a partial overview is built from the legacy `/admin/dashboard/stats` with a 'Limited telemetry' chip. **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean + vitest 497/497 incl. 18 new tests; live curl as the super admin (all three ranges, 422 on `range=14d`, and the three controls against a throwaway `ZZ-THROWAWAY-TENANT` — all cascade-deleted afterwards: tenants/subscriptions counts restored, 0 throwaway tenants left; the 5 control audit rows are append-only and still appear in the activity feed); super-admin tsc/eslint clean; **real browser check as the super admin at 1920/1440/1024/390 px** (no horizontal scroll, boot-sequence frames 1.5 s apart prove the animation, console + Suspend confirmation opened then cancelled — no mutation run against a real tenant) and I viewed the 1440 and 390 screenshots myself: layout renders as designed. **Not verified**: the extend-trial/maintenance/force-logout/reset-password/impersonate buttons through the UI (only via API on the throwaway tenant); the 1024px two-column layout and 360px width visually (overflow checked only); permission-degrade paths; the `Not-yet-used` countries and plan-mix panels (empty datasets here → hidden). **Known cosmetic limits**: the draggable AI launcher can sit over the reactor corner labels/revenue panel corner; with this tiny dev dataset (1 tenant, no revenue) several panels look sparse; the existing `useHasPermission` Redux selector logs a pre-existing new-reference warning. **Dev login**: the super admin in this DB is `admin@appkraft.info` (the user shared the password in chat for testing; it is not recorded here).

121 — **Tenant `/maintenance` screen redesign (tenant-web only)**. The old screen was a plain `StatusScreen` card inside the login split-screen layout, with a made-up `status.fitcloud.com` link and the now-false claim that "member check-in continues to work offline" (maintenance mode, added in Prompt 120, blocks every tenant-plane request). Now: the route lives in its own group `app/(status)/maintenance/page.tsx` + `app/(status)/layout.tsx` (URL unchanged; the old `(auth)/maintenance` was removed) and `features/auth/components/status/maintenance-view.tsx` renders a full-screen tenant-branded page (brand gradient, animated gears + rocking wrench in pure CSS — framer-motion `rotate` on SVG `<g>` did not animate, `animate-spin` does; reduced motion honoured; 'Scheduled maintenance' badge; 3 status stage cards; countdown ring). It polls the public `GET /public/tenants/resolve?slug=` every 30 s (paused while the tab is hidden; reachable during maintenance) with a 'Check now' button; when `maintenanceMode` turns false it shows 'We're back online!' and sends the visitor to `/` after 2.5 s; visiting `/maintenance` when the tenant is NOT in maintenance now shows 'No maintenance in progress' + a button back to the app (before, it wrongly claimed maintenance). No effect fires a mutation. The sibling status screens (account suspended, subscription/trial expired, access denied, session expired) still use the old plain `StatusScreen` look. **Verification**: tenant-web tsc/eslint clean; browser check on a throwaway tenant (`zz-throwaway-maint`, SQL-created, deleted with its Redis keys; real tenant never put into maintenance) at 1440 and 390, light and dark: maintenance, 'Check now' notice, back-online and idle states all rendered; two bugs fixed (dark-mode badge text unreadable; gear animation not running). I viewed the screenshots. **Not verified**: the automatic 30 s poll (only 'Check now' exercised), the 2.5 s redirect destination (`waitForURL('/')` timed out — `/` probably redirects on to a login route), very long tenant names wrap awkwardly in the title.

122 — **Super admin: professional theme + shell + dashboard (replaces the rejected JARVIS HUD) and extended overview API**. User feedback on 120: "too much JARVIS, not a dashboard; I need a professional theme but more controller, charts and reporting on the respective pages" → they asked for a design first (added as a new row of boards, `SaDashboard.dc.html`, in the existing "Payments Redesign" canvas artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/JLTPWhZnop9hg6Q5dsyR9b — NOT a new artifact; the "Staff and Access Redesign" artifact is a single page and can't take boards), approved only the dashboard ("show me dashboard first"), then asked for it to be built. `SaTenants.dc.html` (Tenants list) and `SaTenant.dc.html` (tenant control center) are written in the scratchpad but NOT published/approved; a Revenue & Reports board is not written yet — **the other super-admin pages still await design approval**. **API** (`admin-dashboard`, additive, no migration): `range` also accepts `12m` (365 days); `churnedDaily` (same definition as `kpis.churned`, a tenant counted once on its first day so the series sums to the KPI); `trialFunnel:{started,activated,converted,expired}|null` for subscriptions created in the window with a `trial_ends_at` (activated = `members` usage metric > 0; converted = ACTIVE now; expired = trial ended and not ACTIVE); `trialsExpiring[]` gain `members`/`plan`; `failedPaymentsList` (latest FAILED platform payment per tenant in the window, max 8, with plan/amount/reason/attempt; needs `payments:read`); `topTenants[]` gain `mrr`/`branches` and now rank ALL non-deleted tenants by mrr then members (members may be null; the query reads up to 200 tenants and sorts in JS); `kpis.arpa` (MRR ÷ ACTIVE subscriptions; `previous` always null). **`mrrDaily`/`mrrDailyPrevious` are always null by design**: there is no activation timestamp, a plan change overwrites `planId` (past prices lost), ended states only have `updated_at`, and `subscription_history` is written by only some paths and is empty in dev — real MRR history needs a daily MRR snapshot table (not built). **Super-admin web**: new fonts via `next/font/google` (IBM Plex Sans + Mono, CSP-safe), professional tokens in globals.css (teal primary #0f766e / accent #14b8a6, cool-grey page, retuned `--chart-*`, proper dark variant); shared shell `features/shell/components/*` for EVERY page: 232px graphite sidebar with 4 groups holding all 15 routes (nav filtered by admin permission keys, Dashboard never hidden; count badges for tenants/failed payments/open tickets from the cached overview, hidden when unavailable), top bar with breadcrumb, Ctrl/Cmd+K tenant palette (navigates to `/tenants/[id]`), env badge that says 'all systems normal' only when `health` supports it, theme toggle, sign out; below 768px the sidebar is a hamburger drawer (it used to be simply hidden). Dashboard (`features/dashboard/components/*`): header with 7D/30D/90D/12M, refresh, client-side CSV 'Export report'; command bar with tenant search + quick actions that act on the SELECTED tenant (none selected → focuses search with 'Select a tenant first'; 'Change plan' links to the tenant page); six KPI cards (deltas only where `previous` exists, else a caption such as 'run-rate'); 'Collected revenue' chart this vs previous (not MRR — no history); tenant-status donut + plan-mix; signups vs churn bars bucketed daily/weekly/monthly; trial funnel; trials ending soon (Extend), failed payments (real rows; 'View tenant' links only — Remind/Retry/Send-link/Nudge were NOT built because no backing endpoints exist), at-risk tenants; top tenants by MRR; countries (hidden when empty); recent activity; system health (DB/Redis latency + a rolling 'since page opened' sparkline, job queue); support summary. Dropped from the mock: 'Schedule weekly summary' (no feature), notifications bell (no inbox), API p95/error-rate tiles (no data), ARPA series toggle, owner-email search (search is name/slug), merged 'Plans & coupons'/'Revenue & reports' nav entries (all 15 routes kept). **Tenant control = an INLINE card under the command bar, NOT a drawer** (user: "inline panel, no overlay"; they had complained the console kept opening on its own): `tenant-control-card.tsx` renders only while a tenant is selected (via search, recent chip, or clicking a tenant in trials/at-risk/top-tenants lists), is closed only by Close/×, persists nothing across navigation and is keyed by tenant id so polling/refetch never resets or re-opens it; groups Status & access (suspend/reactivate, maintenance, force logout, reset owner password, impersonate), Subscription (extend-trial stepper 1–90 + reason, TRIAL tenants only), Key facts; every confirmation is an inline row (typed slug where required) — there are no modal dialogs on the dashboard; gating `tenants:read` to view / `tenants:manage` to act; mutations fire only on click. All JARVIS code (24 `hud/*` files, hud-theme.css, `data-hud`, boot sequence, particles, arc reactor, terminal feed, modal confirm dialog) was deleted. Money is ₹ compact (the data may be multi-currency; revenue is summed across currencies without conversion, as elsewhere). Note MRR counts only ACTIVE paid subscriptions, so the dashboard MRR (₹0 on real dev data) differs from the Revenue page (₹17,999, which still counts the trial subscription). **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean + vitest 505/505, live curl of 7d/30d/90d/12m (+ 422 on `6m`) with throwaway tenants; super-admin tsc/eslint clean; tenant-web tsc clean; real browser checks as the super admin (1440 light/dark, 1920, 1024, 390, 360 drawer/palette/inline card/inline confirmation (cancelled), other pages /tenants, /tenants/[id], /revenue, /support, /plans, /payments, /scheduler legible in the new shell) — zero console errors/≥400 responses; I viewed the 1440 light screenshot with the inline card myself. Throwaway `ZZ-THROWAWAY-*` tenants/subscriptions/usage/payments were inserted for the charts and deleted — re-verified: tenants 1, subscriptions 1, 0 throwaway rows (admin audit rows are append-only). **Not verified**: executing any tenant mutation through the UI (by design), churn bars with real churn data, non-zero 'Converted' funnel, permission-degrade paths for restricted admins (read from code only), the final layout fixes made after the last screenshots (reset-password button and change-plan button overflow in the card; top-tenants width at 1024; confirmed by typecheck/lint only), `pnpm build`, 360px for every page.

123 — **Super admin: Tenants list + Tenant detail (Overview, Reports, Usage & limits, Modules, Subscription, Billing, Users, Activity, Support) + admin-plane API**. Designs approved first in the existing "Payments Redesign" canvas artifact (boards `SaTenants`, `SaTenant`, `SaTenantReports`). **Migrations (additive, hand-written; run `prisma migrate deploy` on production)**: `20261004100000_tenant_admin_controls` (`tenant_limits.overrides jsonb`, `tenant_modules.admin_override bool`, new `tenant_admin_notes` and `tenant_admin_tags`) and `20261004100100_enable_rls_tenant_admin_controls` (the two new tables use an admin-plane RLS policy `app.admin_plane='on'`, so tenant/member sessions see 0 rows — probed in a rolled-back transaction with a non-bypass role; the dev DB role is superuser/bypassrls so real RLS enforcement was only tested that way). **API — list** (`admin-tenants`, new files `tenant-list.*`, `tenant-bulk.service.ts`, `tenant-health.util.ts`): `GET /admin/tenants` gained filters `plan, country, createdFrom/To, health (at_risk|fair|healthy), view (trials_ending|at_risk|past_due|suspended|near_limits), tag`, `sort` (mrr|createdAt|lastActiveAt|members|health|name)+`sortDir`, search also matches owner email; items gain planId/planName/mrr/members/membersLimit/branches/country/renewsAt/lastActiveAt/healthScore/healthComponents/atRiskReason/maintenanceMode/tags; response gains `counts`. `GET /admin/tenants/insights` (growth 12 months, planMix, healthDistribution, renewals next 8 weeks; `signupsBySource` is always null — the schema stores no signup source), `GET /admin/tenants/tags`, `GET /admin/tenants/export` (CSV ≤5000 rows, formula-injection safe, audited `admin.tenants_exported`, Tags column), `POST /admin/tenants/bulk` (extend-trial/change-plan/maintenance/suspend/reactivate/force-logout, 1..100 tenants, each through the existing single-tenant function with its own audit row + one `admin.tenant_bulk_action` summary; change-plan also needs `payments:manage`; `mode` defaults to payment_link while `manual` records a paid MANUAL payment per tenant; delete is rejected). Health = `round(0.40·engagement + 0.30·billing + 0.15·utilisation + 0.15·support)` (buckets at_risk 0–40, fair 41–70, healthy 71–100; `computeHealth` is shared with the detail overview); the list and detail now use the SAME live member/staff/branch counts and EFFECTIVE limits (overrides applied), verified equal live; `lastActiveAt` = greater of latest staff login and latest attendance check-in (member-app logins excluded). **API — detail** (`tenant-detail.*`, `tenant-reports.*`, `tenant-controls`, `tenant-notes` services): `GET /admin/tenants/:id/overview`, `/reports?range=30d|90d|6m|12m&compare=` (member growth, feature adoption, check-in heatmap — UTC, weekday 0=Monday, 168 cells, revenue by month = the tenant's PLATFORM payments to FitCloud (not member payments), payment methods, retention cohorts (M0 forced to 100), staff logins by role, tickets by month, plan utilisation + projection; retention90d = share of members who joined in the window ending 90 days ago that are ACTIVE today), `GET/PUT /limits` (null clears an override; **overrides survive plan changes**, in both the admin and tenant-checkout change-plan paths), `GET /modules` + `PUT /modules/:key` (really flips `requireModuleEnabled` for that tenant; guarded modules `dashboard, members, staff, branches` cannot be disabled → 422), `GET/POST/DELETE /notes`, `GET/PUT /tags` (normalised lowercase slugs), `GET /users`, `GET /activity` (the filtered audit trail — the existing `/audit-logs` is unchanged because the current UI expects a plain array there), `GET /support/tickets` (+ counts), `GET /subscription/history`. Browser back returns to the previous page, not the previous tab (tabs use `router.replace`). **Verification**: API tsc/eslint clean, vitest 570/570; super-admin tsc/eslint clean; tenant-web tsc clean; live curl of every list/insight/bulk/export/detail/reports/limits/modules/notes endpoint on throwaway tenants (override → enforcement change and module toggle → module-disabled 403 proven live); real browser checks as the super admin (list at 1440 light/dark, 1920, 1024, 390; detail Overview/Reports/Usage/Modules at 1440 light+dark, 1920, 1024, 390; every tab; inline confirms; limits/notes/tags saved; print preview) — no console errors/≥400 responses except a 409 on 'Resend link' for a seeded payment without a real Razorpay link; I viewed the list and detail Overview screenshots myself and they match the approved designs. No schema or migration changes. **API — plans** (`admin-plans`, new files `plan-insights.*`, `plan-duplicate.service.ts`): `GET /admin/plans` gained `search`/`status` + per-plan `stats{activeSubscribers,trialSubscribers,pastDueSubscribers,mrr,share}`; `GET /admin/plans/overview` (KPIs, `mrrByCurrency`, `byPlan`, `priceLadder` with yearly-discount %, 8-week `upcomingRenewals`), `GET /admin/plans/:id/overview` (stats, subscribers by status/cycle, renewals, `recentChanges` from the audit log — audit has no before/after snapshot — and `couponsUsed`), read-only `GET /admin/plans/:id/impact?priceMonthly=&priceYearly=` (verified: renewals re-read the plan by slug and charge its CURRENT price, so a price change reaches existing subscribers at their next renewal; inactive plans cannot be self-service renewed; coupons are not re-applied at renewal), `POST /admin/plans/:id/duplicate` (inactive copy, unique `-copy`/`-copy-2` slug). Hard-delete still wipes redemption history and nulls `Invoice.couponId` (UI warns and prefers Disable). **API — revenue/payments**: `GET /admin/revenue/summary` MRR/ARR now count **ACTIVE subscriptions only** (previously ACTIVE+TRIALING — now consistent with dashboard/tenants/plans; numbers on the Revenue page DROP by the trial share; trial value is exposed as `mrrTrialing`, plus `mrrByCurrency`), `/revenue/growth` validates `days` 1..366 and takes `currency`, new `GET /admin/revenue/overview?range=30d|90d|12m` (KPIs vs previous, daily/monthly collected, mrrByPlan, by gateway/currency/country, status mix, failure reasons, dunning list, invoice aging, top tenants; `mrrHistory`/`newVsChurnedMrr` are null — no MRR snapshot table exists; multi-currency data is reported in the dominant currency with a `currencyNote`), `GET /admin/revenue/export` (CSV, audited); `GET /admin/payments` gained filters (provider, tenant, date, amount, currency, mode, sort) + `counts` + `summary` (date filter matches `paidAt`, falling back to `createdAt`), `GET /admin/payments/overview`, `GET /admin/payments/export` (CSV ≤10,000 rows, audited), `GET /admin/payments/invoices` filters/counts/summary, new `GET /admin/payments/invoices/:invoiceId`, `GET /admin/payments/:paymentId` + `timeline`. **Security fix**: `transactions[].rawPayload` and `metadata` were previously returned raw (webhook payloads can carry email/contact/VPA/card details) — they are now masked (`[masked]`, partial email hiding). CSV exports are formula-injection safe. **NOT built (no model/permission/testable gateway)**: refunds, retry-charge, mark-paid; the pages say so honestly. **Super-admin web**: `/plans` (KPIs, MRR by plan, subscribers by plan, price ladder, renewals; cards/table toggle; inline create/edit panel with a live price-impact preview, duplicate, enable/disable, typed-slug delete that shows the 409 inline; the form saves `isActive` with a second `setActive` call because the API body doesn't carry it) and `/plans/[planId]` (KPIs, status/cycle charts, read-only price simulator showing the API note, coupons used, audit timeline, paginated subscribers with an inline Change-plan panel on the existing `useChangePlan` hook, still gated by `payments:manage`); `/coupons` (KPIs, 30-day redemptions chart, by type, top, expiring soon, URL-synced filters, usage bars, inline create/edit with live preview, enable/disable (optimistic), typed-code delete explaining the hard-delete, inline bulk generate with copy-all/CSV) and NEW `/coupons/[couponId]` (stats with progress ring, redemptions chart, top tenants, invoices, last-50 history with a drift note); `/revenue` (banner with 30D/90D/12M + compare + server CSV + print-to-PDF, tabs Overview / Plans & MRR / Geography & gateways / Collections, URL-synced `?range=`/`?tab=`, 'Not tracked yet' card for MRR history/new-vs-churned; no refunds figure); `/payments` (KPIs, daily succeeded-vs-failed, status/provider/mode/failure charts, stuck-pending and recent-failure lists, Payments and Invoices tabs with URL-synced filters, count chips, filtered-summary footer, mixed-currency chip, server CSV for payments — disabled on the Invoices tab because the export covers payments only) and NEW `/payments/[paymentId]` (status timeline, webhook log with masked payload, inline Check-status/Resend for PENDING via the existing tenant-scoped hooks, gated by `payments:manage`) and `/payments/invoices/[invoiceId]` (line items, totals, coupon chip, linked payments, PDF, inline-confirmed email to owner). Dropped (no backing data): plan versioning/grandfathering, plan restriction and start date on coupons, per-redemption discount amounts, an attempts count, per-KPI sparklines, before/after diffs.
